Then read the hidden-state probe section and the synthetic-to-real transfer section to understand why the authors argue for two distinct regimes and why common answer-first synthetic constructions are misleading.

If time is short, prioritize four items: the evidence for the label-semantics mismatch, the overall AUROC comparison against the correctness oracle, the stratified detector results for correct versus incorrect answers, and the probe-transfer results. Expected reading time is about 30 minutes. If you work on reasoning supervision or faithful-CoT data construction, the experimental setup and appendix statistics are worth a closer read.43:T435,RouteBench constructs episodes with a support set and a query. The serialization is kept fixed, while the latent regime changes. The solver bank contains four clean representatives of different inductive biases: ridge-like solvers for global shrinkage, lasso-like solvers for sparsity, Huber-like solvers for robustness to heavy-tailed noise, and kNN-like solvers for locality. Route labels are never given to the dense Transformers. At evaluation time, the model answer is compared with the outputs of these solver families, and the closest solver family defines a behavioral route proxy. The paper then checks whether this proxy satisfies structural necessity, nuisance invariance, and causal editability. The baselines include fixed solver representatives, a global mixture, an input-conditioned soft mixture, an unsupervised Gumbel router, privileged-statistic routers, and an oracle. Additional controls include natural-language renderings, a unified four-way setting, route-margin checks, probe permutation controls, matched patching controls, and Switch-MoE diagnostics.44:T5f7,实验使用五个 7–9B instruction-tuned open models：Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Gemma-2-9B、OLMo-2-7B。论文训练 leave-one-out atlases，并分别审计 held-out Mistral 和 Qwen target。第一组是受控 LoRA hidden objectives：向 held-out target 注入三个目标，包括虚构甜点品牌、托勒密本轮体系、Flying Spaghetti Monster advocacy。对六组 objective-target 配对，runtime intervention 能把 planted-objective rate 降到 0，同时 matched benign controls 保持 0。residual dictionary 在全部六组中都把注入机制恢复为 top-ranked latent，3 个种子、2 个目标家族下 mean rank = 1.0，rank 标准差为 0。Mistral 侧的 per-target SAE 和 pairwise crosscoder baseline 在同样对照中没有恢复该机制。第二组是 Qwen 相对 Atlas B 的 panel-relative finding：Qwen-2.5 的 attachment KL 约为 reference panel in-panel KL 的 7 倍，并且跨 3 个 atlas training seeds 高于 in-panel 值超过 15σ。residual channel 将差异定位到 emergency-power restrictions、unrest 中 media-suspension framing、以及弱化 individual rights 的 law-and-order justifications 等 continuation cluster。沿同一 cluster 做 runtime intervention，会单调改变 political-keyword refusal rate 和 law-and-order framing share，100 个 matched out-of-domain controls 保持不变。论文也明确说明该发现是相对于 Atlas B panel 和审计 prompt distribution 的结果，不应解释为 Qwen 的模型内在属性结论。45:T408,The method trains a reference feature atlas, a sparse feature library learned jointly over a panel of previously studied models. Once the atlas is trained and curated, a new target model is attached by fitting only a linear target decoder into the fixed atlas coordinates. This produces two audit views. The atlas channel reads the target through already-interpreted panel features and enables feature-by-feature comparison against the reference models. The residual channel trains a separate sparse dictionary on what the atlas fails to reconstruct, namely the residual h* - D* z_A. This differs from sparse crosscoders, which typically place shared and target-specific features in one dictionary and separate them later using decoder-norm asymmetries. Here, residual membership is architectural: the feature lives in a dictionary trained only on panel-uncovered residuals. Attachment FVU/KL gives a scalar measure of how far the target sits outside the panel, while the residual SAE provides the inventory of responsible features.46:T609,The experiments use five 7–9B instruction-tuned open models: Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Gemma-2-9B, and OLMo-2-7B. The authors train leave-one-out atlases and audit held-out Mistral and Qwen targets. In the controlled setting, they inject three LoRA hidden objectives into the held-out targets: a fictional dessert brand, Ptolemaic epicycles, and Flying Spaghetti Monster advocacy. Across all six objective-target pairs, a runtime intervention reduces the planted-objective rate to zero while matched benign controls remain at zero. The residual dictionary recovers the injected mechanism as the top-ranked latent in every paired run, with mean rank 1.0 and rank standard deviation 0 across the reported seeds and target families. On the Mistral side, per-target SAE and pairwise crosscoder baselines do not recover the mechanism in the same head-to-head comparison. In the panel-relative Qwen audit, Qwen-2.5 attaches to Atlas B at roughly 7 times the in-panel KL and more than 15 standard deviations above the in-panel value across three atlas-training seeds. The residual channel localizes the gap to continuation clusters involving emergency-power restrictions, media-suspension framing during unrest, and law-and-order justifications that down-weight individual rights. Intervening along the same cluster monotonically changes political-keyword refusal and law-and-order framing rates, while 100 matched out-of-domain controls are unchanged. The authors frame this as a panel-relative finding, not a model-intrinsic claim.47:T4c0,The benchmark evaluates 8 models from two providers, including 4 frontier models and 4 production-tier models. On straightforward multi-route eligibility logic, models perform well: 100% accuracy on 43 English-language scenarios, with a Wilson 95% confidence interval of [91.8%, 100%]. Performance drops in nested exception-chain settings. In the March 2026 baseline, Claude Opus 4.6 scores 61/68, or 89.7%, on the spacecraft section, with a confidence interval of [80.2%, 94.9%]. On the 7 failing scenarios at that snapshot, 10 repeated runs per scenario produce 0 correct answers out of 70; the one-sided Clopper–Pearson 95% upper bound on per-trial success probability is 4.19%, indicating systematic rather than stochastic failure. An enhanced prompt does not fix the issue: it lowers net accuracy to 64.7%, with a confidence interval of [52.8%, 75.0%], and introduces 20 false positives. The paper also documents silent model drift: GPT-5.4 on construction insurance moves from 96.6% to 100% under the same prompt and harness without a version bump. The Aethis module matches the formal rule fixtures on all 225 scenarios, with sub-millisecond evaluation latency after compilation and near-zero marginal cost.48:T510,First, it formally proves three core properties of the Entropic Bound in a linear attention surrogate: rank-deficient models incur unavoidable excess risk, the task noise floor is achievable at the intrinsic rank r*, and gradient descent from small initialization converges to r* via implicit bias.
Next, using a controlled interpolation ladder that swaps architectural components one at a time from the linear surrogate to full attention, it localizes static rank failure to the input-conditioned bilinear form XKX^T used for attention score computation — not softmax, not rank constraints. Since the operator depends on input, static properties of kernel K cannot capture actual task capacity.
It then redefines intrinsic rank in an attention-native way: the minimum query-key kernel rank that solves the target task within the attention function class itself. It also proposes energy effective rank as a robust capacity measure for softmax attention, as it is insensitive to small singular value noise introduced by softmax.
Finally, it characterizes the predictability frontier: exact data-only recovery of r* is possible for linear QK attention (even at scale with unknown V), while only partial energy-rank predictability holds for softmax attention due to nonlinear inversion corruption.49:T569,The method is State Transition Pretraining, or STP. Training data consists of step-level tuples (s_t, a_t, s_{t+1}), which can be extracted from existing trajectories or collected automatically from interactive environments. STP jointly optimizes two objectives. Forward dynamics predicts the next GUI state from the current state and action, effectively an image-text-to-image task. This is meant to build an internal model of how GUI states change under actions such as click, type, or scroll. Inverse dynamics predicts the action from consecutive screenshots, an image-image-to-text task. This forces the model to identify what changed, where the interaction occurred, and which visual details are action-relevant. A notable design choice is the use of a unified multimodal model rather than separate modules for perception, action classification, and state prediction. That allows both objectives to share a common tokenized interface and makes the pretrained model easier to transfer into downstream trajectory fine-tuning. The paper positions STP as initialization rather than replacement: it removes some of the burden from trajectory SFT by learning local structure first. The ablations suggest that forward and inverse dynamics provide complementary signals, and that the gains are not sensitive to whether transitions are sampled as full trajectories or as independent steps.4a:T40f,The main results cover both desktop and mobile GUI benchmarks. On AgentNetBench, under matched trajectory fine-tuning settings, STP improves average success rate by 2.3% to 6.2% and coordinate success rate by 1.6% to 6.8%, depending on the fine-tuning split. On AndroidControl, it improves step success rate by 0.9% and coordinate prediction accuracy by 1.3%. On GUIOdyssey, it improves step success rate by 0.6% and coordinate prediction accuracy by 1.6%. The paper also provides optimization evidence: the left panel of Figure 1 shows better cross-entropy behavior during downstream trajectory fine-tuning when initialized with STP. The scalability results are important. With fine-tuning data fixed, increasing transition-pretraining data steadily improves downstream performance. With STP fixed, scaling trajectory fine-tuning data from 2K to 18K still leaves STP ahead of the fine-tune-only baseline. The same quality signal, if reused across all stages, can repeatedly suppress the same domains and styles. Anyone working on pretraining data should immediately benchmark against its multi-objective sampler design."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Peiguang Li","Yongwei Zhou","Juncheng Diao","Yuchun Fan","Jian Yang","Jianxiao Yang","Zhongda Su","Shuguang Jiao","Xiao Wei","Zhiye Zou","Gan Dong","Zhizhao Zeng","Rongxiang Weng","Jingang Wang","Xunliang Cai"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["pretraining-data","web-corpus","data-curation","multi-objective-optimization","deduplication"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22662","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.22662","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.23362","title":"Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-match Tokenization","abstract":"Recent work has shown that subword vocabularies can be trained to optimize compression for a specific inference rule rather than relying on greedy heuristics such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE). We extend this approach to greedy left-to-right longest-match decoding, the fast and widely used inference rule underlying WordPiece. We introduce Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-Match Tokenization (JOLT), which formulates vocabulary learning as an integer program over vocabulary-selection and segment","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-25T21:01:14Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2607_23362.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个很具体但长期被启发式方法主导的问题：当部署时采用 WordPiece 常见的 greedy left-to-right longest-match（GL2R）解码规则时，怎样直接为这个推理规则学习词表，而不是像 BPE 那样先用贪心 merge 过程构造词表，再把它拿去给另一套推理规则使用。难点不在“压缩目标”本身，而在“词表选择”和“分词可实现性”是耦合的：某个切分方案只有在不存在更长 token 抢占边界时，才真能被 GL2R 解码复现。论文要解决的核心，就是把这种 greedy 一致性精确写进优化问题里，使训练时优化的 token 数与部署时真实产生的 token 数一致。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有两层。第一层是 tokenizer 设计范式：词表训练不必绑定某个历史启发式，而应当围绕部署时的 inference rule 直接建模。JOLT 把“tokenizer 训练目标”和“tokenizer 推理规则”对齐，这一点对后续研究很重要。第二层是负结果同样有价值：在 GL2R 这个广泛使用的规则下，BPE 在训练集上的 token count 已经离 LP 下界只有 1%–2% 左右，JOLT 虽然能系统性改进，但 held-out 压缩提升最多只有 0.78%。这说明在固定 byte-level、固定词表规模、固定贪心最长匹配规则的设定下，单靠更强的词表优化，剩余空间并不大。对做大模型预训练的人来说，这个结论比单纯“又快了 0.x%”更重要，因为它帮助判断 tokenizer 研究的边际收益在哪里。","technical_approach":"方法上，JOLT 延续了 ToaST 和 ConvexTok 的“整数规划 → 线性松弛 → rounding”路线，但关键区别是它不是为 split-tree 或 shortest-path 推理设计，而是专门为 GL2R 设计约束。建模对象有两类变量：一类表示候选 token 是否进入词表，另一类表示每个 pretoken 选用哪一种候选 segmentation。目标函数是最小化按频次加权的总 token 数，也就是压缩目标。真正的技术核心在 greedy-consistency constraints：如果某个 pretoken 选择了一个 segmentation，那么在每个 segment 起点处，不能存在一个更长、且同样匹配该位置字节串的已选 token，否则 GL2R 实际会优先输出那个更长 token，训练时选的 segmentation 就不可实现。这个约束把“分词方案”和“词表内容”紧密耦合起来。\n\n另一个关键点是规模控制。因为一个长度为 n 的 pretoken 有 2^(n-1) 种切分，不能全部显式枚举。JOLT 采用按需生成候选 segmentation 的做法，先只保留少量低阶切分，再在求解过程中逐步加入需要的候选，避免组合爆炸。训练语料先按 regex 切成 pretoken，并聚合相同 pretokens 的频次；实际优化只保留 top-N 高频 pretokens。求解时先做 LP relaxation，再通过 rounding 得到固定大小词表。论文强调 rounding 不是主要误差来源，因为最终离 LP bound 很近。和 BPE 相比，JOLT 不再依赖局部 merge 频次；和 UnigramLM 相比，它不是概率生成式分词；和 ConvexTok/ToaST 相比，它的可行性条件是由 GL2R 的最长匹配机制决定的，而不是最短路或树结构推理。","experiments":"实验重点不是下游任务，而是压缩质量、LP 下界诊断和 rounding 损失。作者在四个训练 scope 上评估，N 取 100k、200k、300k、400k 高频 pretokens。结果是：在相同词表规模下，JOLT 在 held-out 文本上的 greedy-L2R token count 相对 BPE 最多下降 0.78%，而且更大的训练 scope 一般带来持平或更好的压缩。更有信息量的是下界分析：BPE 在训练 scope 上距离 LP lower bound 只有 1%–2%，说明 BPE 在 GL2R 设定下已经相当接近最优；JOLT 能弥合其中 89.6%–99.4% 的 gap；rounding 后的离散词表距离 LP bound 仅 0.008%–0.176%，几乎没有额外损失。这个结果直接支持作者的判断：JOLT 相对 BPE 的提升小，不是因为 rounding 做坏了，而是因为理论可提升空间本来就不大。\n\n如果从预训练角度读实验，还应注意两点。第一，论文讨论的是 token count compression，而不是 LM perplexity 或下游精度，因此不能自动推出“训练更好”；它更像是在给 tokenizer 设计空间做上界测量。第二，作者使用 byte-level 设定并保留单字节 token，因此不存在 OOV 问题，实验结论主要反映“在这个基础设定下，GL2R 词表优化的纯压缩上限”。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和方法总览，抓住一句话：JOLT 的价值不只是多压缩一点，而是把 GL2R 的真实解码约束写进优化目标。然后重点看定义 greedy-consistency 的部分，这里是全文最值得读的地方；如果这个约束没看明白，整篇论文会显得只是“又一个 LP tokenizer”。接着看实验里关于 LP lower bound、BPE gap、rounding gap 的表和图，这些结果比 headline 提升更有判断价值。附录里如果给了 separation / column generation / rounding 细节，也值得快速扫一遍，因为这决定方法是否能扩到更大语料。\n\n预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。若只想判断是否值得继续深挖，看三件事就够：GL2R 可实现性约束怎么写；BPE 离下界到底多近；JOLT 的收益是否主要来自更大训练 scope 而非更复杂 rounding。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a precise tokenizer-design problem: how to learn a vocabulary that is directly optimized for greedy left-to-right longest-match decoding (GL2R), the inference rule commonly associated with WordPiece, instead of training a vocabulary with a heuristic such as BPE and only later decoding with GL2R. The hard part is not compression alone. Under GL2R, a segmentation is valid only if no longer selected token can preempt any segment boundary. So vocabulary selection and segmentation choice are coupled. The paper’s central contribution is to encode this coupling exactly, so the optimization objective matches the token count realized at deployment.","importance":"For pretraining research, the main value is conceptual. It reinforces that tokenizer training should be aligned with the actual inference rule, rather than inherited from historical heuristics. JOLT is a clean example of inference-aware tokenizer optimization. The second contribution is diagnostic rather than purely incremental: under byte-level tokenization, fixed vocabulary size, and GL2R decoding, BPE is already within roughly 1%–2% of the LP lower bound on the training scope. JOLT improves on BPE consistently, but the held-out gain tops out at 0.78%. That is a useful boundary result. It suggests that, in this regime, better vocabulary optimization alone has limited remaining headroom. For people working on foundation-model pretraining, that matters more than the raw compression delta.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"$10","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and the problem setup. The key idea to retain is simple: JOLT matters because it optimizes for the actual GL2R decoding rule, not because it beats BPE by a large margin. Then go directly to the section defining the greedy-consistency constraints. That is the part worth understanding in detail; without it, the paper can look like just another LP tokenizer paper. After that, read the experimental tables and figures on the LP lower bound, the BPE gap, and the rounding gap. Those diagnostics are more informative than the headline compression gain.\n\nIf the appendix includes the separation procedure, candidate-generation details, or rounding algorithm, it is worth skimming because those details determine whether the method can scale beyond the reported setup. We propose DataOrchestra, a framework that unifies different processing operations and orchestrates an example-specific pipeline for each example. Given a chunk of pretraining data, an orchestrator decides whet","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-27T17:54:12Z","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2607_24717.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作解决的是预训练数据清洗中的“逐样本决策”问题：不同文本块需要不同处理，有的应丢弃，有的应保持不动，有的只需去噪，有的需要重写或教学化增强。固定清洗流水线会浪费算力，也容易把高质量样本改坏；单一 Open models have recently advanced quickly in test-time scaling, RL, and reasoning, but many pretraining foundations remain around the 1T-parameter regime. That limits the ceiling of downstream RL and agentic training. K3 aims to push several constraints at once: a 2.8T-class MoE foundation, 1M-token context, native multimodality, and long-horizon RL, while keeping the whole stack trainable and deployable.","importance":"This report matters because it provides a relatively complete recipe for how an open 3T-class foundation is actually built. Its importance comes in three layers. First, it is not just parameter scaling. The report combines long-sequence modeling, deep information flow, extremely sparse MoE stabilization, and data/training recipe refinements, and attributes an overall ~2.5x scaling-efficiency gain over Kimi K2 to that combination. That is directly relevant to scaling-law and systems-aware pretraining research. Second, it treats pretraining and post-training as one loop: the foundation is designed for 1M-context agentic RL, and RL capabilities are later consolidated back into a unified model through multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Third, it shows that at this scale, architecture, infrastructure, and RL systems cannot be studied in isolation if one wants to understand the final capability-cost tradeoff.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"$16","reading_guide":"$17","authors_note":"Moonshot AI / Kimi technical report. We present DynaResize, a runtime GPU reallocation system that dynamically switches GPUs between Rollout and Training to balance stage execution times without changing RL semantics. DynaResize decomposes resizing into fine-grained operations and removes non-startup-critical work from the critical","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-15T09:41:09Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22614.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"DynaResize 解决的是 RL post-training 系统中的资源错配问题：Rollout 和 Training 分离部署后，静态 GPU 分配会被 rollout 长尾延迟拖慢，训练侧出现 pipeline bubble；截断 rollout 或接受 stale policy 可以缓解吞吐，但会改变 RL 语义。","importance":"这篇工作和基座预训练的关系在于 post-train 基础设施。大规模 RL 已经成为 reasoning model 训练的重要环节，rollout 生成长度高度不稳定，系统吞吐会直接限制实验规模和迭代速度。DynaResize 的价值是把资源调整放在系统层处理，而不是牺牲算法语义。对做 RL pretrain、R1-Zero 类训练、长 CoT rollout 的团队，核心启发是：瓶颈不只在模型或奖励，也在 rollout-training 两阶段的动态负载匹配。","technical_approach":"DynaResize 在运行时动态把 GPU 在 Rollout 和 Training 两种角色之间切换。它没有减少单条 rollout 的延迟，而是让集群资源跟随当前瓶颈移动。直接切换 GPU 角色会遇到三个问题：通信拓扑重建耗时，参数和优化器状态迁移可能造成瞬时显存放大，频繁响应短期波动会导致调度抖动。DynaResize 将 resizing 拆成细粒度操作，分析依赖关系，把非启动关键路径上的工作提前、缓存或延后。三个主要机制是 communicator reuse、bounded state staging 和 hysteresis resizing。前者缓存并预热通信元数据；第二个用有界 host-memory buffer 流式搬运权重和优化器状态，并延迟非必要 optimizer state reload；第三个只在持续负载不平衡时触发 resizing，避免频繁切换。","experiments":"相对最优静态 GPU 配置，DynaResize 报告端到端 post-training 吞吐提升 66.5%，总执行时间减少 33%（以 100-step baseline 归一化）。角色切换相关开销降低或隐藏 27%。这些结果说明收益主要来自减少 rollout 长尾导致的训练侧空转，而不是提升单步生成速度。论文也强调该系统可以和截断、异步 RL 等算法层方法组合，但单独使用时不改变原始 RL 语义。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–25 分钟。重点看 Background and Motivation 中对 disaggregated rollout/training pipeline bubble 的分析，以及三类替代方案的取舍：partial rollout、fully asynchronous RL、静态 partition。方法部分重点看 resizing 被拆成哪些操作，哪些在 critical path，哪些可以 prewarm 或 defer。评估部分优先看相对最优静态配置的吞吐、总时间和切换开销 breakdown。若负责 RL 训练平台，需要细读 communicator reuse 和 state staging 的实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"DynaResize targets resource imbalance in disaggregated RL post-training. Rollout and Training are placed on separate GPU pools, but static partitioning suffers when rollout latency has a long tail. Training workers can sit idle, and algorithmic fixes such as rollout truncation or stale-policy updates may change the intended RL semantics.","importance":"The relevance is infrastructure for large-scale post-training rather than model architecture. For reasoning-model RL, long chain-of-thought rollouts create highly variable generation times, and throughput can become a hard limit on experiment scale. DynaResize shows that part of this problem can be handled at the system layer by dynamically matching GPU allocation to the active bottleneck, without changing the RL algorithm.","technical_approach":"DynaResize dynamically switches GPUs between Rollout and Training roles at runtime. It does not make individual rollouts faster; it improves cluster utilization by reallocating GPUs when one stage becomes the bottleneck. A naive design would be blocked by expensive communication-topology reconstruction, transient memory amplification during parameter and optimizer-state migration, and scheduling thrashing under token-level latency variance. The system decomposes resizing into fine-grained operations, analyzes dependencies, and moves non-startup-critical work off the critical path. Its main mechanisms are communicator reuse, bounded state staging, and hysteresis-based resizing. Communicator reuse caches and prewarms topology-scoped communication metadata. State staging streams weights and optimizer states through bounded host-memory buffers and defers non-critical optimizer-state loading. Hysteresis avoids reacting to short-lived imbalance and triggers resizing only under sustained pressure.","experiments":"Compared with the best static GPU partition, DynaResize improves end-to-end post-training throughput by 66.5% and reduces total execution time by 33% on a normalized 100-step baseline. It also hides or reduces 27% of role-switching overhead. The gains come from reducing pipeline bubbles caused by rollout stragglers, not from reducing per-sample generation latency. The system is presented as orthogonal to algorithmic methods such as truncation or asynchronous RL.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–25 minutes. Read the motivation section for the pipeline-bubble analysis in disaggregated rollout/training setups. Then focus on the resizing design: which operations are on the critical path, which can be prewarmed, and which can be deferred. In the evaluation, prioritize throughput, total-time, and role-switch overhead breakdowns against the best static configuration. For an affine map $z=Wx+b$ with input mean $μ$, a weight update contains a sample-independent displacement $ΔWμ$ that is functionally indistinguishable from a bias update. We call this hidden contribution \\emph{boundary motion} and decompose each update into a centered, sample-varying \\emph{shape} component and a shared \\emph{bo","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-24T21:51:43Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22927.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer优化中通常将权重和偏置作为独立参数张量分别优化，但当仿射层输入均值非零时，权重更新中隐含的样本无关位移项ΔWμ与偏置更新在函数层面完全等价，现有优化器无法区分这两种功能重叠的更新路径，可能导致优化效率低下。","importance":"揭示了标准AdamW优化器中被忽略的隐藏边界运动通道，为理解Transformer训练动态、优化参数空间与函数空间的对齐关系提供了新的分析视角，对优化器设计有理论参考价值。","technical_approach":"提出仿射层梯度与更新的形状-边界分解：将权重更新拆分为依赖样本的形状分量（作用于输入的中心化偏差x-μ）和共享的边界分量（ΔWμ，与偏置更新功能等价），二者在经验输出空间内积下严格正交。设计诊断用优化器SBO-AdamW，为形状梯度和边界梯度分别维护独立的Adam状态，将权重中的偏置类分量剥离后单独优化边界坐标。","experiments":"在IMDb数据集上训练4层Transformer编码器，17个仿射模块、301个检查点的统计显示，ΔWμ的范数通常比显式偏置更新大两个数量级以上，几乎贡献了全部的实际边界位移。SBO-AdamW原型显著加快了拟合速度，提升了验证集选择的测试集结果。同时发现使用小批量移动中心进行分解会导致偏置补偿更新过大和参数规范漂移，是该方法的关键失效模式。","reading_guide":"泛读为主，重点看第2章的形状-边界分解推导和第4章的实验测量结果，预计阅读时间15-20分钟。从事优化器研究的读者可以关注其提出的函数空间正交化思路和失效模式分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In Transformer optimization, weights and biases are typically optimized as separate parameter tensors, but when the input to an affine layer has non-zero mean, the sample-independent displacement term ΔWμ implicit in weight updates is functionally identical to bias updates. Standard optimizers cannot distinguish these two functionally overlapping update paths, potentially leading to inefficient optimization.","importance":"Reveals the overlooked hidden boundary motion channel in standard AdamW, provides a new analytical perspective for understanding Transformer training dynamics and aligning parameter-space optimization with function-space effects, and has theoretical reference value for optimizer design.","technical_approach":"Proposes a shape-boundary decomposition of affine layer gradients and updates: weight updates are split into a sample-dependent shape component (acting on centered input deviations x-μ) and a shared boundary component (ΔWμ, functionally equivalent to bias updates), which are exactly orthogonal under the empirical output-space inner product. A diagnostic optimizer SBO-AdamW is implemented, maintaining separate Adam states for shape and boundary gradients, stripping the bias-like component from weight updates and optimizing the boundary coordinate independently.","experiments":"Measured on a 4-layer Transformer encoder trained on IMDb. Across 17 affine modules and 301 checkpoints, the norm of ΔWμ is typically over two orders of magnitude larger than explicit bias updates, accounting for nearly all actual boundary displacement. The SBO-AdamW prototype substantially accelerates fitting and improves validation-selected test performance. A critical failure mode is identified: decomposition with a moving minibatch center causes large compensating bias updates and parameter gauge drift.","reading_guide":"Skim-level read. Focus on Section 2 for the shape-boundary decomposition derivation and Section 4 for experimental measurement results; estimated reading time 15-20 minutes. For optimizer researchers, the function-space orthogonalization idea and failure mode analysis are worth deeper attention.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"揭示AdamW下affine层权重与偏置的优化冗余，§3的分解和实验值得在预训练优化消融中参考。","en":"Uncovers optimization redundancy between affine weights and biases under AdamW; §3 decomposition and experiments are worth referencing in pretraining optimization ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhang Gongyue","Sheng Yixuan","Liu donghan","Wang Zhiyong","Ren Weihong","Liu honghai"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["transformer optimization","adamw","affine layer","function space decomposition"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22927","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.22927","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}}]},{"id":"architecture","emoji":"🏗️","name":"LLM 架构与建模","description":"LLM 架构、MoE/SSM/混合架构、注意力结构、Diffusion/Flow/Masked/Any-order LM 等语言建模范式","papers":[{"id":"2607.24507","title":"UNIFUSION: Adapting Autoregressive Language Models into Discrete Diffusion under a Unified Reverse-Rate Objective","abstract":"Existing methods mainly adapt pretrained autoregressive (AR) language models to masked diffusion, whereas we directly adapt them to uniform-noise diffusion, where every token remains editable during sampling. However, adapting AR checkpoints across corruption kernels remains challenging because existing DLMs use different objectives and prediction parameterizations. We establish connections among SEDD, MDLM/GIDD, M2S, and Neural CTMC by expressing their conditional losses as a single generalized","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-27T14:40:26Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_24507.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何将预训练自回归（AR）语言模型直接适配为均匀噪声离散扩散模型，而无需经过掩码扩散中间阶段。现有 AR 转扩散的方法（DiffuGPT、DiffuLLaMA、Dream-7B）都基于吸收掩码核，只能编辑仍处于掩码状态的位置，采样步数增加时会出现 unigram 熵下降、重复模板等退化问题；均匀噪声核允许所有位置全程可编辑，但不同离散扩散方法（SEDD、MDLM/GIDD、M2S、Neural CTMC）的目标函数和参数化方式差异大，难以直接复用 AR 权重。","importance":"该工作在理论层面统一了四种主流离散扩散方法的条件损失，证明它们在反向速率空间中等价于同一个广义 KL 目标；在工程层面提出了 AR 到均匀扩散的直接适配方案 Unifusion，跳过了掩码扩散中间阶段。对预训练研究的价值在于：它给出了一个清晰的 x₀ 预测接口，使得 AR 模型的语言知识可以更高效地迁移到不同扩散核（吸收/均匀），为后续统一 AR 与扩散建模范式提供了理论和实践基础。","technical_approach":"核心分为两部分：\n1. 反向速率空间统一：将 SEDD（concrete score）、MDLM/GIDD（clean token）、M2S（posterior mean）、Neural CTMC（exit rate + jump distribution）四种方法的输出都映射到反向跳变速率，证明它们的条件损失等价于同一个广义 KL 目标。进一步推导出从 clean-token 预测到另外三种参数化形式的转换公式，使得同一份 x₀ 预测可以产生完全相同的反向速率。\n2. Unifusion 适配方案：利用 AR 模型的 softmax 输出天然就是下一位置 clean token 预测这一性质，将输出位置右移一位，直接作为扩散模型的 x₀ 预测器初始化。微调过程中逐步将因果注意力放开为双向注意力，加入时间条件嵌入，使用线性均匀噪声调度。整个过程不需要先训练掩码扩散模型。","experiments":"在 GPT2-small 和 GPT2-medium 两个规模上验证，主要结论：\n1. 四种速率等价的原生目标函数，从相同 GPT2-small 初始化出发，在有限训练预算下会走出不同的优化轨迹，说明速率空间等价不代表训练动态等价。\n2. AR 直接转均匀扩散（Unifusion）达到的最低 GenPPL 与先转掩码扩散再转均匀扩散的两阶段路径相当。\n3. 在 5 次种子实验中，Unifusion-S 和 Unifusion-M 随着采样步数增加，GenPPL 和 unigram 熵同时提升（掩码扩散模型往往出现 GenPPL 降但熵也降的退化）。256 步时，Unifusion-S 的 GenPPL/熵为 97.783/5.2626，Unifusion-M 为 71.516/5.6669。\n4. 在 WinoGrande、SIQA、BBH 零样本评测上，两个规模的 Unifusion 都在对比的扩散模型中取得最高准确率。","reading_guide":"精读第 3.1 节反向速率空间统一的推导，特别是定理 3.5 关于 x₀ 预测作为统一接口的部分，这是全文核心理论贡献。第 3.2 节 Unifusion 的具体适配流程（注意力展开、时间条件、初始化方式）是工程实现关键。第 4.3 节关于 GenPPL-熵 trade-off 的分析值得细读，它揭示了掩码扩散和均匀扩散在采样质量上的本质差异。附录中的转换公式推导可按需深入。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to directly adapt pretrained autoregressive (AR) language models to uniform-noise discrete diffusion models, without going through an intermediate masked diffusion stage. Existing AR-to-diffusion methods (DiffuGPT, DiffuLLaMA, Dream-7B) all use absorbing mask kernels and can only edit positions that remain masked; as sampling steps increase, they suffer from degraded unigram entropy and repetitive templates. Uniform-noise kernels keep all positions editable throughout sampling, but different discrete diffusion methods (SEDD, MDLM/GIDD, M2S, Neural CTMC) use disparate objectives and parameterizations, making direct AR weight reuse difficult.","importance":"Theoretically, this work unifies the conditional losses of four mainstream discrete diffusion methods, proving they are equivalent to the same generalized KL objective in reverse-rate space. Practically, it proposes Unifusion, a direct AR-to-uniform-diffusion adaptation scheme that skips the intermediate masked diffusion stage. For pretraining research, it provides a clean x₀ prediction interface that allows linguistic knowledge from AR models to be efficiently transferred to different diffusion kernels (absorbing/uniform), laying both theoretical and practical groundwork for unifying AR and diffusion modeling paradigms.","technical_approach":"The work has two core parts:\n1. Unification in reverse-rate space: The outputs of SEDD (concrete score), MDLM/GIDD (clean token), M2S (posterior mean), and Neural CTMC (exit rate + jump distribution) are all mapped to reverse jump rates. Their conditional losses are shown to be equivalent to the same generalized KL objective. Conversion formulas from clean-token predictions to the other three parameterizations are derived, so that the same x₀ prediction produces identical reverse rates.\n2. Unifusion adaptation: Leveraging the property that an AR model's softmax output is naturally a clean-token prediction for the next position, outputs are shifted right by one position to directly initialize the diffusion model's x₀ predictor. During fine-tuning, causal attention is gradually opened to bidirectional attention, time conditioning is added, and a linear uniform noise schedule is used. No intermediate masked diffusion checkpoint is required.","experiments":"Verified on GPT2-small and GPT2-medium scales, with key findings:\n1. The four rate-equivalent native objectives follow different optimization trajectories under finite training budgets from the same GPT2-small initialization, showing that rate-space equivalence does not imply equivalent training dynamics.\n2. Direct AR-to-uniform diffusion (Unifusion) reaches a minimum GenPPL comparable to the two-stage path of masked diffusion followed by uniform diffusion.\n3. Across five seeds, Unifusion-S and Unifusion-M improve both GenPPL and unigram entropy as sampling budget grows (masked diffusion models often show the degenerate pattern of lower GenPPL paired with lower entropy). At 256 steps, Unifusion-S achieves GenPPL/entropy of 97.783/5.2626, and Unifusion-M achieves 71.516/5.6669.\n4. On zero-shot WinoGrande, SIQA, and BBH benchmarks, both Unifusion scales achieve the highest accuracy among compared diffusion models.","reading_guide":"Read Section 3.1 closely for the reverse-rate unification derivation, especially Theorem 3.5 on x₀ prediction as a unified interface — this is the core theoretical contribution. Section 3.2 on the Unifusion adaptation pipeline (attention unwinding, time conditioning, initialization) is the key engineering detail. Section 4.3's analysis of the GenPPL-entropy trade-off is particularly revealing, as it exposes a fundamental quality difference between masked and uniform diffusion sampling. Appendix derivations of conversion formulas can be explored as needed.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"统一了离散扩散LM各分支的损失与参数化接口，给出AR权重转uniform扩散的可落地recipe，做非AR建模的团队应立刻复现验证权重复用率","en":"Unifies loss and parameterization interfaces across discrete diffusion LM variants, provides a practical recipe to convert AR checkpoints to uniform diffusion; non-AR LM teams should replicate to verify weight reuse rates."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Xiaoyi Jiang","Jingyuan Li","Yixuan Jiang","Wei Liu","Yi Zhu","Zuoqiang Shi","Pipi Hu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["discrete diffusion LM","autoregressive adaptation","unified objective","continual pretraining","non-autoregressive LM"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24507","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.24507","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.24176","title":"Where Quality Breaks in Compressed Short-Text Generation: Staged Bottleneck Localization","abstract":"Compressed short-text generators can fail in two different places: the codec may discard information before generation starts, or the latent generator may produce weak codes. It aims to extend conditional computation from FFN-only MoE layers to the whole LLM pipeline, while avoiding the overhead and instability seen in prior pipeline-level mixture methods.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining research is architectural. The paper proposes a horizontal capacity-scaling mechanism where entire streams or sub-model pipelines are mixed, rather than only replacing FFNs with experts. Its current impact should be read as an architecture exploration with useful design signals. The experiments are on GPT-2-style models trained on FineWeb, so they support feasibility and scaling trends, but do not yet settle whether this approach beats mature large-scale MoE training recipes.","technical_approach":"The central idea is a formal equivalence between MoE-style mixture layers and dynamic convolutions with input-dependent kernels. Each expert can be interpreted as a 1×1 convolutional kernel, and the router produces input-dependent weights for mixing those kernels. Based on this view, the authors introduce cMoLLM. The model keeps multiple end-to-end streams, associates each stream with a distinct 1×1 kernel, uses a lightweight gating network to produce soft mixture weights, and implements the final mixing through grouped pointwise convolution. Unlike Top-K MoE, it uses fully differentiable soft gating. Unlike ParaScale, it does not add virtual tokens and therefore avoids increasing sequence length. Unlike AltUp, it does not rely on an auxiliary prediction branch.","experiments":"The empirical study uses GPT-2-style models trained on FineWeb, with evaluations on perplexity, GLUE, and SQuAD. The paper reports that cMoLLM matches or improves over dense baselines under comparable cost, and shows better stream utilization, more stable training dynamics, and favorable scaling compared with ParaScale-style and AltUp-style pipeline mixtures. The provided text does not include exact parameter counts, training-token budgets, perplexity values, or task-level gains; these should be checked in Section 5.3 and the appendix tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. scaling：模型越大、参数先验越强，外部知识服从性可能越差。这提醒基座模型并不天然更容易被上下文覆盖。","technical_approach":"TokenMem 由三部分组成。第一是 TokenMemoryBank，用 FAISS 存储和检索知识 passage。第二是在冻结 LLM 每一层加入 cross-attention adapter，把检索知识通过独立通道注入。关键点是 cross-attention 对外部知识单独做 softmax，不和原 self-attention 中的 query/context token 争同一个归一化预算。adapter 通过零初始化 gate 接入残差流，训练开始时不破坏原模型行为。第三是两阶段 CoT curriculum SFT，只训练约 3–7M 参数的薄 gate/adapter。Phase 1 训练一般知识利用能力，Phase 2 用 counterfactual knowledge 强化冲突场景下的服从。这个设计和普通 prompt-RAG 的区别在于外部知识有独立注意力通道；和模型编辑或 LoRA 微调的区别在于主模型参数保持冻结，知识更新主要发生在外部 memory 和轻量注入模块上。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个模型、3 个模型家族：Qwen3-4B/8B/14B、LLaMA-3.1-8B、OLMo-3-7B。评估重点是冲突样本上的 Knowledge Compliance，使用 greedy 输出是否匹配 passage-supported answer 来计数。论文报告了几个关键现象：vanilla RAG 在知识冲突下存在参数记忆偏置，并且在更大模型上 KC 更低；仅有 cross-attention 通道还不够，Phase 1 只训练知识利用时 KC 会接近崩塌，说明架构给了容量，但训练信号必须显式覆盖反事实服从；两阶段训练后，gate adapter 会形成按冲突程度和层位置变化的注入策略。给出的材料没有包含完整表格数值，具体 KC 提升幅度、各模型分项和消融数字需要核对实验表。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。先看 §3.1 对标准 RAG attention 竞争的形式化描述，这是论文最有信息量的部分。然后看 cross-attention adapter 和 zero-initialized gate 的接入方式，重点判断它是否真的隔离了知识通道，还是只是在残差流中延后融合。实验部分优先看三类结果：vanilla RAG 的 inverse scaling、Phase 1/Phase 2 curriculum 消融、gate 的层级分析。若关注 continual learning 或外部知识写入，KC 指标定义也值得单独记录。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TokenMem addresses a specific failure mode of frozen LLMs under retrieval augmentation: when retrieved knowledge conflicts with the model’s parametric memory, the model may ignore the retrieved passage even if that passage is treated as authoritative. The paper frames this as Knowledge Compliance rather than ordinary factual accuracy. The architectural claim is that standard RAG routes retrieved tokens and query tokens through the same self-attention softmax, so external evidence competes with strong parametric priors for a fixed attention budget.","importance":"The work is not a core pretraining recipe, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it studies the interface between frozen parametric knowledge and writable external memory. It also separates controllability from truthfulness: in some systems, the desired behavior is to follow the designated memory entry, even when it contradicts what the model has learned. The reported inverse-scaling behavior in vanilla RAG is an important warning: stronger parametric memory can make a model less compliant with external evidence.","technical_approach":"TokenMem combines a FAISS-backed TokenMemoryBank, a layer-wise cross-attention adapter, and a two-phase SFT curriculum. Retrieved passages are injected through a separate cross-attention channel, with its own normalization, instead of being concatenated into the prompt and processed only by self-attention. The adapter is gated and zero-initialized, so the frozen backbone is initially preserved. Only a small adapter of roughly 3–7M parameters is trained. The curriculum first teaches general knowledge use, then trains on counterfactual conflicts to force compliance with the injected passage. This differs from prompt-only RAG by removing the direct attention-budget competition, and differs from model editing by leaving the main model weights frozen.","experiments":"The experiments cover five models from three families: Qwen3-4B/8B/14B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, and OLMo-3-7B. The main metric is Knowledge Compliance on conflicting examples, measured by whether greedy decoding follows the passage-supported answer. The paper reports that vanilla RAG shows a bias toward parametric memory and that larger models can be less compliant. It also reports that Phase 1 alone is insufficient: knowledge-utilization training without explicit counterfactual compliance can drive KC close to zero even with the cross-attention channel present. The adapter analysis suggests conflict-aware and layer-specific gating behavior. Exact per-model numbers and gain sizes are not available in the supplied content and should be checked in the result tables.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25–35 minutes. To address these limitations, we propose TR","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-29T00:47:48Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22661.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"D-LLM 的幻觉检测不能直接照搬自回归 LLM 的不确定性指标，因为扩散式生成同时有空间维度的多位置更新和时间维度的多步去噪。TRE 解决的是：不训练额外检测器、不做多次采样，只用单次生成过程里的熵信号判断幻觉风险。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在幻觉检测本身，而在于提供了一种观察 diffusion LM 解码动态的简单诊断信号。AR 模型的 token entropy 只有一个 next-token 前沿，D-LLM 的 entropy 是 token position × denoising step 的二维结构。TRE 的设计说明，D-LLM 的可靠性信号应优先看“正在被揭示的 token”和“后期去噪步骤”，这对分析 diffusion LM 的训练—推理不匹配、置信度校准、解码策略都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法是参数-free、single-run 的 Temporal-weighted Revealing Entropy。核心分两步。第一步按每个去噪 step 中 token 的 revelation state 对 token 分组，经验分析发现最有用的不是已经稳定的 token，也不是尚未被模型实质决定的位置，而是当前 step 正在 reveal 的 token；这些 token 对应模型正在提交的内容决定，其 entropy 更接近事实性风险。第二步沿时间聚合 entropy evidence。作者观察到后期 denoising step 对幻觉检测更有判别力，因此给后期步骤更高权重，形成 temporal-weighted 的熵分数。它和训练式 D-LLM 检测器的区别在于不拟合 action trace、deviation model 或动态图网络，也不需要标注数据和额外 detector forward。","experiments":"实验覆盖多个数据集和多个 D-LLM backbone，用 TRE 与已有训练式检测方法以及面向 AR LLM 的 uncertainty baseline 比较。给出的结论是 TRE 在检测效果、计算开销和跨设置鲁棒性上表现稳定。片段中没有提供完整表格数字，因此不应把具体提升幅度外推。已给出的关键信息是：TRE 不需要 detector training，不需要 repeated sampling，只依赖一次生成中的 entropy trace。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 15–25 分钟。重点看三处：第一，Introduction 中对 D-LLM 幻觉检测为什么不同于 AR LLM 的问题拆解；第二，token revelation state 的分组定义和 revealing-token entropy 的经验分析；第三，temporal weighting 的设计依据。若关注 diffusion LM 的训练动态，可以进一步看 entropy 随 denoising step 变化的图，因为这类曲线比最终检测指标更有研究价值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets hallucination detection for diffusion language models without training an auxiliary detector. The key issue is that D-LLMs expose uncertainty over both token positions and denoising steps, so AR-style next-token uncertainty metrics are not directly aligned with the generation process.","importance":"Its main relevance to pretraining research is diagnostic rather than the hallucination task itself. TRE offers a lightweight way to inspect D-LLM decoding dynamics. The useful signal is not generic token entropy, but entropy on tokens being revealed at a given step, with later denoising steps carrying more detection value. This is useful for studying confidence calibration, decoding behavior, and possible train–inference mismatch in diffusion LMs.","technical_approach":"TRE, Temporal-weighted Revealing Entropy, is parameter-free and uses a single generation run. It first categorizes tokens by their revelation state at each denoising step and keeps entropy from revealing tokens, since those positions correspond to active content decisions. It then aggregates these step-level entropy signals with a temporal weighting scheme that emphasizes later denoising steps. This differs from prior D-LLM hallucination detectors based on action-trace modeling, deviation learning, or dynamic graph modeling, all of which require labeled data and an additional trained detector.","experiments":"The experiments evaluate TRE on multiple hallucination datasets and multiple D-LLM backbones, comparing against training-based D-LLM detectors and uncertainty-style baselines adapted from AR LLMs. The reported finding is that TRE is competitive while being cheaper and more portable, since it avoids detector training and repeated sampling. s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-29T06:24:19Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22663.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理 ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-27T15:58:07Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_24593.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 token-level sparse attention 在真实长上下文推理里的一个新瓶颈：主注意力已经从全量 attention 降到只看 top-k token，但用于选出这 top-k 的 indexer 仍然要对每个 query 扫完整个前缀，单层复杂度还是 O(L^2)。论文抓住的不是 token 轴、head 轴或 layer 轴，而是 query 轴上的冗余：相邻 query 往往会选到高度重合的 top-k token，因此没必要为每个 query 各做一次完整前缀扫描。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在预训练方法本身，而在长上下文 serving 与稀疏注意力落地。很多稀疏 attention 方案论文里看起来便宜，真正上线时却被索引阶段拖住，这篇工作把瓶颈拆得很清楚，也给出一个不改模型、不重训、可直接替换的方案。它的价值主要有两点：第一，说明 sparse attention 的效率分析不能只看 downstream attention kernel，还要把 candidate selection 算进去；第二，提出 query-group 共享索引这一条独立于 token/head/layer 压缩的新轴，对后续训练期联合设计也有启发，比如是否可以在预训练中显式鼓励局部 query 的检索一致性，从而让这类近似更稳。","technical_approach":"方法很直接，也很工程化。PIVOT 的核心是把一组相邻 query 合成一个 proxy query，只做一次 full-prefix traversal，然后把这次扫描得到的高分 token 作为整组共享候选集。之后再分两种变体。PIVOT-Refine 会让组内每个 query 只在这个小候选集上重新打分，再取自己的 top-k；这样把原本每个 query 的 O(L) 扫描改成 O(c)，其中 k < c << L。PIVOT-Reuse 更激进，直接把 proxy query 的 top-k 复用给整组 query，连 per-query refine 都省掉。复杂度上，g 个 query 的索引成本从 O(gL) 变成 Refine 的 O(L + gc) 或 Reuse 的 O(L)。论文成立的经验基础有两个：一是相邻 query 的 top-k overlap 很高；二是 indexer score 沿 key 轴呈长尾分布，所以只要 c 比 k 稍大，通常就能覆盖组内大部分真正需要的 token。实现上它保持 DSA 接口不变，下游 Sparse MLA 完全不用改，因此是 drop-in replacement。prefill 阶段按固定组大小分组；decode 阶段则直接利用 multi-token prediction 同一步产生的 d+1 个 token 作为一组，因此能和 MTP 的加速叠加。方法的关键取舍也很清楚：Refine 更稳，Reuse 更便宜；组越大，摊薄越明显，但候选覆盖风险也会上升。","experiments":"论文在 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 GLM-5.1 上做了 LongBench 与 RULER 评测，结论是 Refine 基本能维持与原始 dense DSA indexer 相当的精度，同时显著降低索引开销。文中给出的代表性结果是：在长上下文场景下，indexer operator 最多可加速 4×，端到端延迟最多降低 1.6×。问题背景里的一个数字也很关键：在 200K context 时，原始 indexer 可占 prefill 延迟约 81%，decode 延迟约 41%，这解释了为什么只优化 attention kernel 不够。实验重点不是绝对任务分数提升，而是 accuracy-latency tradeoff：PIVOT-Refine 在较小候选预算下就能接近基线，说明 query-group 的 top-k union 远小于最坏情况 gk；PIVOT-Reuse 则进一步换取速度，但更依赖 query 相似性。消融的核心变量应包括 group size g、candidate size c、Refine vs Reuse、prefill 与 decode 两阶段差异，以及不同长上下文长度下的收益变化。全文给人的信息是：收益主要来自把重复 prefix scan 摊薄，而不是改写下游稀疏 attention 算子。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Figure 2。Figure 2 基本给出了整篇成立的证据：相邻 query 的 top-k overlap 高，而且组内 top-k 并集增长很慢。然后看方法部分里 proxy query 的构造方式、candidate set 的选取、Refine 与 Reuse 的复杂度对比。实验部分重点看三类结果：一是 indexer latency 与 end-to-end latency 的拆分；二是不同 g、c 下的精度保持情况；三是 prefill 和 decode 是否都稳定受益。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要内容。如果只想判断是否值得跟进，实现接口兼容性和 MTP 结合方式最值得看，因为这决定了它是不是一个真实可部署的方案。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical bottleneck in token-level sparse attention. Even if the downstream attention only attends to top-k tokens, the indexer still scores every prefix token for every query, so indexing remains O(L^2) per layer. The key observation is that this work does not reduce cost along the token, head, or layer axis; it attacks redundancy along the query axis. Nearby queries often retrieve highly overlapping top-k tokens, so running a full prefix scan for each query is wasteful.","importance":"Its relevance is mainly on long-context inference and sparse-attention deployment rather than pretraining itself. Many sparse-attention designs look efficient on paper but hide a dense retrieval stage that dominates latency in production. First, sparse-attention efficiency should be evaluated end-to-end, including candidate selection. uncertainty，并显示错误轨迹通常有更多、更早的不确定片段。这类信号可以用于推理数据筛选、过程监督数据构造、RL rollout 诊断，以及分析模型在哪些推理阶段失稳。它更像是 inference-time scaling 的工程化改进，但提供了一个可复用的训练信号视角。","technical_approach":"DeepLook 是一个 training-free 的 monitor-and-intervene decoding 框架。方法先用 top-k token log-probability 统计构造 token-level confidence，再聚合为 segment-level confidence。系统维护最近历史的置信度分布，用局部分位数阈值检测当前 segment 是否出现相对置信度下降。触发后，不是立即生成多条完整答案，而是在当前位置展开若干候选 continuation，并做固定长度 lookahead rollout。分支排序使用 Average Lookahead Confidence，即候选未来若干 segment 的平均置信度。这样选择的不是局部最自信的下一步，而是未来一段推理仍然稳定的分支。框架还包含 adaptive token budget。相对 DeepConf-low，数据集级 token 生成量平均减少 87.3%。在 Qwen3-32B 的 AIME25 上，DeepLook 超过 Cons@512，同时 token 使用量约少 17 倍。动机实验也有信息量：错误轨迹平均包含更多 uncertain segments，并且第一个 uncertain segment Pareto 图，以及 DeepConf-low、self-consistency、Cons@512 的对比。若关心训练数据或 RL rollout，可额外关注 uncertainty signal 是否能转化为过程奖励或样本过滤规则。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"DeepLook addresses compute allocation during reasoning-time decoding. Many wrong reasoning traces show a confidence drop before the final answer becomes visibly wrong. Full self-consistency wastes tokens by regenerating long shared prefixes, while next-step confidence alone is too myopic.","importance":"The paper is not a pretraining recipe, but it is useful for reasoning-model research. It shows that segment-level uncertainty, derived from token log-probabilities, can localize fragile parts of a reasoning trace. This signal could be used for rollout filtering, process-supervision data construction, RL diagnostics, or failure analysis of reasoning models. Its main value is a practical view of how internal confidence dynamics correlate with reasoning failure.","technical_approach":"DeepLook is a training-free monitor-and-intervene decoding framework. It converts top-k token log-probability statistics into token-level confidence and aggregates them into segment-level confidence. A local percentile threshold over recent history is used to detect relative confidence drops. When an uncertainty bottleneck is detected, the decoder branches at that point and performs fixed-horizon lookahead rollouts. Branches are ranked by Average Lookahead Confidence, the mean confidence over future rollout segments. This differs from selecting the locally most confident next token: it asks whether a continuation remains stable over the near future. Adaptive pruning further reduces token use by stopping weak branches early.","experiments":"The evaluation uses four competition-level math benchmarks: AIME24, AIME25, BRUMO25, and HMMT25. Models include DeepSeek-R1-8B, Qwen3-32B, GPT-OSS-20B, and GPT-OSS-120B. DeepLook matches or exceeds strong baselines in most settings while using roughly 10%–20% of their token budget. It reduces dataset-level token generation by 87.3% on average compared with DeepConf-low. On AIME25 with Qwen3-32B, it outperforms Cons@512 while using about 17 times fewer tokens. The diagnostic analysis is also important: incorrect traces contain more uncertain segments and encounter the first uncertain segment earlier.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Existing diffusion-based drafting often follows a single linear path, even though a diffusion model produces token distributions for many positions in parallel. This leaves much of the candidate space unused. A naive tree extension is also imperfect: diffusion scores are position-wise marginal probabilities, while AR verification accepts tokens under prefix-conditioned probabilities. The ranking signal and the verifier’s decision rule are therefore misaligned.","importance":"The paper is relevant to both diffusion language modeling and inference efficiency. It shows that the serving advantage of diffusion LMs is not only about parallel token prediction; it also depends on how candidate distributions are converted into paths that an AR-style verifier can accept. The key lesson is that well-calibrated marginals are not enough when the acceptance process is sequential and prefix-dependent.","technical_approach":"PRESTO introduces prefix-aligned tree drafting. It modifies diffusion marginal scores with a prefix-dependent correction, aiming to preserve the useful signal from the diffusion drafter while making path ranking closer to the AR verifier’s acceptance behavior. A priority-based tree search then expands high-potential candidate paths under a fixed drafting budget. Compared with AR tree drafting, the method has to compensate for the absence of native left-to-right conditional probabilities. Compared with linear diffusion drafting, it uses the multi-candidate structure that diffusion generation already exposes.","experiments":"The paper evaluates PRESTO in two settings: dedicated diffusion drafters for AR LLM speculative decoding, such as the dFlash line of work, and self-speculative decoding for diffusion LMs. The reported outcomes are higher accepted length and better end-to-end throughput. Furthermore, thanks to decentralization, PTStore allows the expansion ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-25T21:51:36Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22648.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有 prefix KV cache 主要局限在单 GPU 或单节点内复用，在多节点大规模 serving 中无法充分复用热门前缀，也容易让持有热门 prefix 的节点成为瓶颈。","importance":"这篇论文属于推理系统方向，对预训练方法本身影响有限，但对长上下文和高复用 workload 的基座部署有参考价值。它关注的是 KV cache 从“本地运行时优化”变成“分布式存储与复制问题”之后的设计取舍：缓存粒度、元数据查询、热门前缀复制、容量和带宽之间的平衡。这类系统约束会反过来影响长上下文模型的服务成本评估。","technical_approach":"PTStore 借鉴 CDN 的思路，把可复用的 KV cache prefix 作为分布式对象管理。核心设计有三点。第一，用 tensor-level 的细粒度增量存储表示 prefix，让多个请求共享公共前缀，同时允许后续分支像 trie 一样无重复扩展。第二，用合并后的元数据支持一次性 prefix 查询，避免在分布式 trie 上逐跳查找带来的延迟。第三，对热门 prefix 做复制，让访问更靠近发起请求的 GPU 或节点，并缓解少数节点因热门 tensor 被频繁读取而形成的负载不均。系统目标不是替代 vLLM 这类运行时，而是作为分布式 prefix cache 层，与现有 serving runtime 集成。","experiments":"摘要和正文介绍部分给出的主要结果是：在长文档问答类 workload 上，PTStore 相比不聚合跨节点、跨 GPU KV cache 内存的基线，可实现约 5–6 倍更高的执行效率。论文还强调其在并发场景下能降低 I/O 开销和端到端运行时间。由于提供内容没有展开完整实验表格，具体集群规模、模型规模、prompt 长度分布、缓存命中率和带宽利用率需要直接查 Section 6。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。先看 Section 4 的设计原则，确认它如何把 prefix cache 抽象成分布式 tensor store。再看 Section 5 的实现细节，重点关注元数据组织、prefix query 路径和与 LLM runtime 的接口。最后看 Section 6 的实验，优先检查三个数字：端到端延迟/吞吐、缓存命中率、网络与存储 I/O 开销。若关心预训练研究，可只读 introduction 和实验设置，用来估算长上下文模型部署时 prefix 复用带来的成本变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Prefix KV caching is effective within a GPU or a node, but large-scale multi-node serving misses many reuse opportunities and can overload the nodes that hold popular prefixes.","importance":"This is an inference-systems paper rather than a pretraining paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but practical: long-context and retrieval-heavy deployments increasingly depend on prefix reuse, and the cost model changes when KV cache becomes a distributed storage and replication problem. The paper is useful for understanding the trade-off among cache granularity, metadata lookup cost, hot-prefix replication, memory capacity, and network bandwidth.","technical_approach":"PTStore treats reusable KV-cache prefixes as distributed tensor objects, following a CDN-like design. The first idea is fine-grained tensor-level incremental storage, so common prefixes can be shared while divergent continuations grow without redundant copies, similar to a trie. The second idea is consolidated metadata, which allows one-shot prefix lookup instead of walking a distributed trie hop by hop. The third idea is popularity-aware replication of hot prefixes, improving locality and reducing load imbalance at the cost of controlled redundancy. The system is positioned as a distributed prefix-cache layer that can integrate with existing LLM serving runtimes rather than replacing them.","experiments":"The reported headline result is a 5–6x efficiency gain on long-passage question-answering workloads compared with baselines that do not aggregate KV-cache memory across nodes and GPUs and therefore often regenerate reusable prefixes. Eviction and episodic-memory schemes therefore rest on a premise rarely tested directly, that a retained event is still informative once the observations that produced it are gone. We test it by omitting one earlier observation from what is served, across otherwise identical agent histories. Among items sensitive to that observation, the answer overwhelmingly fol","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-26T14:37:53Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_23693.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“KV cache 能不能当记忆”这种泛问题，而是一个更细、更关键的 serving 问题：当系统只保留一小部分历史事件对应的 KV 行、并把真正写入信息的源事件删掉之后，被保留的下游事件 KV 是否仍然携带了由已删除上游观测计算出来的状态。作者把这个现象定义为 semantic materialization。核心难点在于，普通准确率无法区分三种来源：答案是否直接写在保留文本里、模型是否靠先验或排除法猜对、还是 KV 行里真的保留了不可见的上游状态。论文因此设计了 donor-pair 因果对照：两段历史在所有被服务的 token 与位置上完全一致，只改变一个已被删除的源事件中的值，看最终回答是否跟着这个被删值变化。这样测到的就是“被保留 KV 行是否独立承载了已消失输入的计算结果”。","importance":"对长上下文 agent、episodic memory、KV eviction、cache-backed memory 这条线，这篇工作的意义在于把一个常被默认成立的前提单独测了出来：保留下来的 KV 行不只代表它们可见文本，还可能携带来自已删除上下文的隐式状态。于是，很多“删掉源事件后性能不降”的结论并不能说明源事件不重要，只能说明某个下游 KV 块已经把信息写穿过去了。这会直接影响 memory policy 的评估方式、cache eviction 的解释、以及长期 agent 里‘记忆单元’应该如何设计。更进一步，作者还说明这种写入不是纯自然涌现、也不是稳定通道，而是一个可编程但容量有限、checkpoint 相关、表面形式敏感的机制。对做预训练和架构的人，这提示了一个值得认真看的方向：Transformer 预填充阶段会把部分中间计算压进后续 token 表示里，而 serving 时这些表示可能成为一种比文本更强、但也更脆弱的记忆接口。","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"最关键的结果来自 donor-pair 因果测试。在 Qwen3-8B 上，针对 donor-sensitive items，最终回答对被删除 source value 与其对照值的跟随比达到 99:0，而被服务文本中没有任何 span 明说哪个值正确。这基本排除了“看见答案文本”的解释，也大幅压低了纯猜测解释。\n\n在可编程写入实验里，作者构造 answer-free carrier 后，Qwen3-8B 上 donor-aligned recovery 从 6% 提升到 51%。更细一点，在固定 query position 的设置下，给同一个 downstream row 旁边加上 carrier，读出分数从 0.00 提高到 0.42，说明 materialization 不是只存在于某个单点 token，而是能通过额外承载事件显著改变可服务状态。\n\n触发条件实验用了 16 种 construction bank。作者报告这些构造在表面理解上平均可被模型正确读取到 0.98，但真正 write-through 效果从 chance 到 0.95 波动很大，而且没有任何一种构造在三种近期模型上都稳定属于强写入类。这说明“模型理解了句子”与“模型把状态写进后续 KV”不是一回事。\n\n容量边界实验也很有信息量。二元状态恢复达到 0.934，对比随机基线 0.5 明显更高；但 4-way、8-way payload 基本靠近各自 chance rate，三位数字 payload 则几乎完全缺失。这个结果把现象从“神秘记忆”拉回到更可信的解释：模型能把少量、压缩过的判别状态写进后续表示，但远不是任意信息都能无损搬运。\n\n论文还做了 landing 和 co-serving 相关分析，显示 root event 是主要承载体，而 reference edge 更像访问路径的一部分；同时同一 row 的可读出性依赖与哪些其他 span 一起被服务。这对 cache eviction 很重要，因为它意味着“保留哪一行”不是独立决策，组合也会改变可恢复信息。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓住实验设计，再看边界条件。优先看 Introduction 里 donor-pair 的定义和 Fig.1，这里把问题设定得很清楚：比较对象不是文本 span，而是被独立服务的 KV block。然后看 §3，理解 99:0 这个主结果是怎么排除文本泄露、先验猜测和 decoy elimination 的。作者专门强调 decoy 要独立采样，这一点值得注意，因为很多类似实验会被“排除错误选项”伪装成“记住正确答案”。\n\n接着看 §4 到 §6。§4 讲 trigger，适合快速建立直觉：materialization 对表面形式很敏感，不能把它当成稳定 API。§5 讲 landing 和 两部分精读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper isolates a serving-time question that is usually assumed but rarely tested directly: if a system keeps only a subset of historical KV blocks and evicts the original source observation, can a retained downstream event still carry state computed from that missing source? The authors call this semantic materialization. The hard part is measurement. Raw accuracy cannot tell whether the model succeeds because the answer is explicitly present in the served text, because it guesses from priors or decoy elimination, or because the retained KV rows genuinely encode hidden upstream state. To separate these cases, the paper uses a donor-pair causal comparison: two histories are byte-identical on all served tokens and positions, and differ only in the value written by an earlier source event that is later omitted from serving. If the final answer tracks that omitted value, the retained downstream KV must be carrying information beyond visible text.","importance":"This matters for long-horizon agents, episodic memory, and KV-eviction systems because it changes how memory ablations should be interpreted. If dropping a source event does not hurt downstream accuracy, that does not prove the source was unnecessary; it may only mean that some retained downstream KV block had already absorbed the relevant state during prefill. That is a different claim, and a much stronger one than text retrieval can support. The paper therefore sharpens the evaluation target for cache-backed memory systems: row selection is not equivalent to text selection. For pretraining and architecture work, the broader implication is that causal prefill can compress some intermediate computation into later token representations, creating a serving interface that is stronger than raw text but also fragile, checkpoint-specific, and capacity-limited.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"$1d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这个结果会改变 KV eviction 的默认心智模型；先看 omission setup，再决定是否内部复现实验。","en":"This shifts the default mental model for KV eviction; read the omission setup first and consider an internal replication."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zefeng Cai","Zerui Cai"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["kv-cache","long-context","agent-memory","serving","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23693","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.23693","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.24667","title":"Eviction as Estimation: A Fixed-Lag Smoothing View of Test-Time Memory, and When Measuring Beats Accumulating","abstract":"A language model with a bounded working memory must repeatedly decide which stored items to keep. However, in practice, models face various unknown memory constraints at deployment. Current adaptive methods compute layer sensitivity assuming upstream layers are in FP16, which mismatches the fully quantized context at deployment, leading to sub-optimal bit allocation.","importance":"Introduces MixQuant, a technique-agnostic adaptive post-training quantization (PTQ) framework. It enables a single offline calibration to serve any memory budget at deployment, solving the 'deployment-context mismatch' in adaptive quantization and improving model accuracy under varying memory constraints.","technical_approach":"The core innovation of MixQuant is computing layer sensitivity (distortion scores) without relying on FP16 upstream layers. Instead, it measures the output distortion of a layer under random mixed-precision configurations of its upstream layers and marginalizes it via Monte Carlo sampling. This yields context-independent distortion scores. Furthermore, MixQuant estimates quantization technique parameters (e.g., AWQ scales) on activations induced by actual allocations and introduces tail regularization to protect low-bitwidth layers. Finally, bit allocation is formulated as a multiple-choice knapsack problem and solved efficiently at deployment using a greedy algorithm.","experiments":"Evaluated across multiple models and datasets, MixQuant consistently outperforms existing adaptive and mixed-precision baselines under various memory budgets. Upstream knowledge selection pipelines (e.g., RAG reranking, GraphRAG graph traversal, confidence estimation) produce rich structured priors, but these are collapsed into a flat token sequence during prompt serialization. The serving backend only sees a linear token stream and loads the full KV-cache uniformly during decoding, so runtime cost scales with serialized context length rather than the small fraction of context actually needed for reasoning. This mismatch worsens as knowledge selection becomes more sophisticated and context grows longer.","importance":"For pretraining research, this work delivers three actionable implications: 1. Long-context pretraining gains require co-design with serving runtime efficiency; simply expanding context window size is offset by linear growth in decoding cost, preventing full realization of performance improvements. 2. Pretraining objectives could be adapted to encode structured knowledge priors (importance, graph structure, confidence) directly into model representations, rather than relying solely on prompt serialization, potentially improving both reasoning quality and inference efficiency. 3. The value of long-context pretraining data should be evaluated alongside runtime knowledge consumption patterns, not just raw context length performance.","technical_approach":"The paper proposes Knowledge Access Planning (KAP), a plan-driven paradigm for runtime knowledge consumption built on a compiler-executor split. A universal intermediate representation called the runtime access plan decouples logical prompt semantics from physical KV-cache access. The KAP compiler translates high-dimensional structured priors from upstream systems (ranked evidence, graph topology, multimodal alignment, confidence scores) into this IR, while the KAP executor interprets the plan to control physical KV access in the serving backend. No changes are required to the knowledge selection frontend, the language model itself, or the logical prompt structure. As a concrete instantiation, GraphSpec implements KAP for GraphRAG frontends and vLLM backends, compiling graph-derived priors into access plans that guide selective KV consumption while retaining full-context verification capability.","experiments":"The provided paper fragment does not include full experimental results. While recent efficient methods attempt to compress tokens via hard pruning or uniform merging, they operate strictly in the spatial feature domain, where robust structural context and discriminative semantic details are inherently entangled. In this work, we propose WaveZip, a joint signal-frequency-domain framework for efficient video inference. Existing token compression methods operate directly in the spatial feature domain: hard pruning breaks spatial coherence, while uniform merging blurs semantically salient regions. The root issue is frequency conflation: in the spatial dimension, task-relevant fine-grained evidence is mixed with redundant background texture; in the temporal dimension, slow-varying semantic trends are mixed with high-frequency fluctuations from sampling noise, visual jitter, and abrupt scene changes.","importance":"This work presents a training-free, plug-and-play video token compression framework built on frequency-domain decomposition. Its core design—allocating token budgets based on separated frequency bands—offers useful reference for handling visual token redundancy in unified multimodal pretraining, designing efficient visual tokenizers, and optimizing long-video modeling efficiency.","technical_approach":"WaveZip uses the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) for spatiotemporal decomposition, chosen for its localized support, invertibility, and multi-band decomposition properties. Temporally, it applies 1D DWT to frame-level query relevance sequences, gates high-frequency detail coefficients with an inter-frame visual change prior, and reconstructs a rectified relevance sequence via inverse DWT to guide adaptive frame-level token budget allocation. Spatially, it applies 2D DWT to per-frame feature maps, retains the low-frequency approximation subband that encodes structural information, and reweights high-frequency detail subbands with query-conditioned spatial saliency. Reconstructed features are downsampled according to allocated budgets and projected to compact visual tokens. The entire pipeline requires no task-specific training and integrates directly with off-the-shelf LVLMs.","experiments":"The experimental section of the available manuscript is truncated. Recent works show the initial success in attributing the con- tributive score of the contexts. However, we observe that when the context overlaps with the training data, these methods can- not disentangle in-context from in-weight (IW) contributions, producing unreliable scores. Based on this observation, in this work, we introduce: 1) an evaluation protocol that relies on","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-26T19:06:29Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_23804.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决的问题是：当上下文里提供的证据与模型参数中已记住的知识（in-weight, IW）发生重叠时，现有 context attribution 方法（基于删除/扰动的敏感性度量）会把“模型本来就会”误判成“上下文不重要”，从而无法在知识层面区分 ICL 贡献与 IW 贡献，导致 attribution 分数在证据支持性判断上失真。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于改进训练，而在于把“训练数据记忆/IW 知识”作为 attribution 评测中的关键混杂因素显式化：1）提醒研究者与工程系统不要把 removal-based attribution 当作证据归因或溯源工具；2）提供一套围绕 IW/ICL 混叠的评测指标与带 provenance 标注的数据集，使得后续工作可以更严谨地讨论“模型已知 vs 上下文提供”的边界。这对训练数据重叠、记忆与可解释性/溯源评测的接口问题有启发。","technical_approach":"方法上主要做两件事：\n1）把 attribution 的语义从“文本片段是否改变输出”提升到“知识是否支持正确答案”。核心观察是：删除某段上下文不改变输出，可能是因为该段无关，也可能是因为同一知识已存在于权重里；两者在 removal-based 框架下不可辨。\n2）提出一套专门测 IW/ICL 混叠的评测协议与指标：\n- BCS（base-model context attribution score）：用 base model（未被注入/未被强化相关 IW 知识的模型）来测上下文 attribution，作为对照，意图把“模型已知”因素最小化。\n- CAC（cross-model attribution consistency）：跨模型一致性，检查当模型的 IW 知识状态变化时，attribution 排名/分数是否稳定；若方法把 IW overlap 当成 irrelevance，会表现出不合理的不一致。\n- APS（attribution preservation score）：在控制 IW 知识变化的条件下，评估 attribution 是否能保持对真正 supporting 片段的高分。\n- SSP（source separation precision）：把任务明确为来源分离（ICL vs IW），直接衡量方法能否把 supporting 但 IW-overlap 的片段与真正 irrelevant 片段区分开。\n3）构建 WMDP-Cyber++：在网络安全/知识问答语境下，为每个 context segment 提供“来源/溯源”层面的 ground-truth（哪些信息应归因于上下文，哪些应归因于权重），用来系统性测上述指标。\n整体上，这篇工作不是提出新的 attribution 算法，而是证明现有方法在 IW overlap 下的结构性失败，并给出能暴露该失败的评测工具。","experiments":"全文主实验围绕四类 attribution 方法（ContextCite、AttriBot、TracLLM、Token-Shapley）在 IW/ICL overlap 控制条件下的表现：\n- 现象复现：当 supporting 片段的知识同时存在于权重中时，方法会给出接近 0 的分数（论文用 pandas 版本差异示例直观展示）。\n- 指标层面：通过 BCS/CAC/APS/SSP 显示这些方法的分数会随“模型是否已知该知识”显著漂移；尤其在 SSP（来源分离）上表现不佳，说明它们不能简单改造为 provenance/溯源工具。\n- 论文强调：传统 top-k log-prob drop、LDS 等 removal-based 评测会掩盖该问题，因为它们与被测方法共享同一类假设（删除导致变化才算贡献）。\n（注：你提供的全文片段未包含完整表格数字与所有模型/设置细节；精读时应以论文结果表为准补齐各指标的具体数值与显著性结论。）","reading_guide":"建议按“问题定义→评测协议→数据集→结果”顺序读：\n- 先看 Introduction + Figure 1：把 IW overlap 导致的语义歧义建立起来，这是全文的主线。\n- 重点读方法部分对四个指标（BCS/CAC/APS/SSP）的形式化定义：它们分别在控制变量上解决什么评测盲点。\n- 精读 WMDP-Cyber++ 的构建与 provenance 标注逻辑：标注粒度、如何定义 IW vs ICL ground-truth、如何控制模型的“已知/未知”。这决定了指标是否可信。\n- 看主结果表：对比同一方法在 base model vs IW-overlap model 下的 attribution 漂移；再看 SSP 相关结果，理解“不能简单适配为来源分离”的证据链。\n预计泛读 30–45 分钟能抓住结论；想复现或用于自家系统评测，需要把指标实现与数据构建细节读透。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode of context attribution under training-data/weight overlap: when the evidence in the prompt context duplicates knowledge already stored in the model weights (in-weight, IW), perturbation/removal-based attribution methods conflate “the model already knows it” with “the context is irrelevant”, producing misleading importance scores and failing to separate ICL contributions from IW contributions at the knowledge level.","importance":"Its main impact on pretraining-oriented research is diagnostic rather than algorithmic: it turns IW knowledge overlap (a byproduct of We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-29T03:13:24Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_22561.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决 LLM-as-a-judge 在大规模评测和偏好标注中的成本、延迟、不可解释和重复推理问题。核心思路是把评测时调用大模型，改成先用大模型合成可执行的 judge 程序，再用程序在本地批量打分。","importance":"它和预训练本身不是同一层问题，但对 post-train 数据生产、reward model 训练、自动评测流水线有实际意义。当前很多 SFT/RL 数据筛选、偏好比较和 reward distillation 都依赖 LLM judge。如果 judge 成本随样本数线性增长，百万级数据处理会很贵，也很难追踪判分逻辑。程序化 judge 的价值在于把一部分评测信号变成可复用、可检查、可编辑的规则集合。它不能替代强模型处理开放复杂案例，但可以承担高频、结构清晰、可规则化的评测子任务。","technical_approach":"系统名为 PAJAMA。流程分三步。第一步，用 LLM 根据不同 rubric 生成一组 Python judge 程序，而不是只生成单个规则。这样做是为了增加判分逻辑的多样性，避免一个程序覆盖不足。第二步，在 held-out validation set 上校准每个程序的输出，筛选有效程序，并把多个程序的 verdict 聚合成最终偏好判断。聚合不是简单投票，因为不同 rubric 的分数尺度和噪声不同，需要建模冲突和置信度。第三步，引入 confidence-aware fallback：当程序委员会没有覆盖输入，或聚合结果置信度低时，再路由给 LLM judge。这个设计承认程序 judge 的边界，把它定位成高吞吐前端，而不是完全替代模型推理。","experiments":"实验覆盖 5 个偏好数据集和 4 个模型家族。独立的 programmatic judges 能达到 OLMo-2-13B-Instruct 级别的准确率，同时运行速度快 47.25 倍。与 LLM fallback 结合后，PAJAMA 改善准确率—吞吐折中：搭配 OLMo-2-7B-Instruct 时，准确率提升 5.0%，吞吐为 2.9 倍；相对 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，准确率提升 2.6%，吞吐为 2.2 倍。在 RewardBench 上，用程序 judge 标签训练的 reward model 超过使用 proprietary LLM 偏好标签训练的模型，API 成本低 50 倍，并且评测阶段不需要 proprietary 调用。工作还测试了偏置样本，结果显示程序 judge 对某些风格偏置更稳；配合 coding agent 迭代校准程序后，鲁棒性进一步提高。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30–40 分钟。重点看 Framework 中程序合成、程序输出校准、committee aggregation 和 fallback router 的设计。实验部分优先看 accuracy-throughput Pareto 曲线、RewardBench reward model 蒸馏结果、biased samples 鲁棒性分析。对做预训练或 post-train 数据流水线的人，最值得看的不是具体 benchmark 分数，而是它把 judge 从黑盒推理变成可缓存、可审计评测资产的工程模式。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles the cost, latency, opacity, and repeated-inference burden of LLM-as-a-judge pipelines. Instead of calling an LLM for every evaluation example, it asks an LLM to synthesize executable judging programs once, then runs those programs locally at scale.","importance":"This is more relevant to evaluation and post-training data pipelines than to base-model pretraining itself. Still, it matters because preference labeling, reward distillation, SFT data filtering, and automated regression tests often rely on LLM judges. If the judge must be re-run for every sample and every rubric change, large-scale data production becomes expensive and hard to audit. Programmatic judges offer a different operating point: reusable, inspectable, editable decision logic for cases where the rubric can be partially expressed as code. They are not a full replacement for strong model judges on open-ended cases, but they can serve as a high-throughput front end.","technical_approach":"The system is called PAJAMA. It has three main components. First, an LLM synthesizes a diverse pool of Python judging programs from rubric prompts. The use of a committee is important because a single generated program usually has limited coverage and brittle logic. Second, the system calibrates program outputs on a held-out validation set, selects useful programs, and aggregates their decisions into a final preference verdict. This is not just majority voting, since different rubrics produce scores with different scales and noise levels. Third, a confidence-aware router sends uncovered or low-confidence examples to an LLM fallback. The design treats programs as a cheap and transparent first pass, while retaining model inference for ambiguous cases.","experiments":"The evaluation uses five preference datasets and four model families. Standalone programmatic judges match the accuracy of OLMo-2-13B-Instruct while running 47.25 times faster. In the hybrid setting, PAJAMA improves the accuracy-throughput trade-off: with OLMo-2-7B-Instruct as fallback, it reports a 5.0% accuracy gain at 2.9 times throughput; compared with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, it reports a 2.6% accuracy gain at 2.2 times throughput. On RewardBench, a reward model trained from programmatic-judge labels outperforms one trained on proprietary LLM preference labels, with 50 times lower API cost and no proprietary calls at evaluation time. The paper also reports robustness tests on biased samples and shows further gains when a coding agent iteratively calibrates the judge programs.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. The useful lesson for LLM research is that safety for tool-using agents cannot be measured only by textual refusal. Once an agent can invoke tools and modify files, the ground truth should include tool traces, runtime behavior, and persistent environment changes. This kind of evidence can inform post-training data, tool-use policies, and permission design for coding agents.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework is an execution-grounded red-team testing setup. It embeds target unsafe operations into plausible engineering workflows using a four-slot template: Motivation, Test Objective, Procedure, and Pass Criterion. The probes are instantiated across unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation workloads. Execution happens inside an isolated Docker sandbox. Outcomes are determined from observable evidence such as tool invocations, runtime traces, and filesystem diffs, not from the agent’s final text. The framework also uses oracle feedback so a red-team agent can refine probes based on observed execution outcomes. Compared with prompt-only jailbreak tests, the object of measurement is actual system-changing behavior.","experiments":"The key demonstrated pattern is the gap between direct unsafe requests and contextualized engineering probes. A direct request to alter startup or configuration files can be refused, with no tool use and no state change. We introduce SQBench, a benchmark for evaluating production-oriented task delivery by language-model agents. SQBench v1.0 contains 220 standardized tasks organized into L1 atomic capabilities, L2 composite skills, and L3 business scenarios. Its value for LLM research lies in how it defines supervision targets for agentic post-training. The unit of evaluation is a deliverable: required files, fields, formats, paths, code behavior, evidence, and permission constraints. This exposes failures that answer-centric benchmarks often miss, such as a semantically reasonable response that cannot be parsed, audited, or consumed by a downstream process. The design is also relevant for reward modeling: task completion and delivery risk are separated instead of collapsed into one score.","technical_approach":"SQBench v1.0 contains 220 standardized tasks organized into L1 atomic capabilities, L2 composite skills, and L3 business scenarios. Each task specifies the instruction, input assets, available tools, execution boundaries, delivery path, scoring rules, and risk-trigger conditions. Functional requirements are scored according to verifiability: deterministic checks are used when possible, while semantic requirements use an LLM judge with predefined rubrics. The scoring pipeline computes Completion first, then applies independently evidenced triggers from a 10D Risk Matrix to derive Risk Penalty and Performance. Strict Pass requires both Completion = 1 and Risk Penalty = 0. The risk dimensions include fabricated facts, format violations, unauthorized actions, tool misuse, and forgotten objectives.","experiments":"The benchmark is run on 27 model configurations. Decision-Making，包括判断当前上下文是否足够、是否需要外部搜索、如何分解和改写查询。第二是 Information Synthesis & Verification，包括来源权威性、时效性、跨源一致性、矛盾检测、多跳证据整合和拒绝干扰项。为了让这些能力可控，论文提出 document-grounded reverse engineering 的合成流程：先从高质量真实文档出发，把它们视为完整证据回看；再反向生成自然查询、答案、局部支持证据和所需推理结构。随后保留主题相关的真实噪声文档，并构造证据锚定的干扰项，例如改变来源权威性、发布时间、实体或版本。最后用多步过滤保证任务可回答、答案不容易靠参数记忆得到、证据标签正确、干扰项确实具有迷惑性。这个设计和普通从 query 出发再跑检索的 benchmark
