We study hallucination detection from the geometry of hidden activations and introduce the D-Score, a simple spectral statistic computed from a single forward pass. For a fixed model, layer, and tolerance parameter, the D-Score counts how many singular directions of the hidden activation matrix have singular v","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-27T15:52:33Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_24586.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作研究能否只用一次前向传播中的 hidden states，检测一段文本是否包含模型内部不支持的 claim。它不依赖检索、不采样多次回答，也不训练额外分类器，而是把幻觉检测问题转成 hidden-state trajectory 的谱结构检测。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对基座模型研究有两个启发。第一，hidden states 的几何形状可能包含比最终 token 分布更直接的可靠性信号，可以作为训练动态、后训练对齐和安全评测的辅助观测。第二，D-Score 这种训练-free、单次前向的标量信号，适合做轻量监控或离线分析。它的贡献量级应保持克制：它提供了一个简单可复现的内部状态指标，但还没有证明该指标能稳定跨模型、跨层、跨长度、跨任务泛化，也没有直接给出如何改进预训练目标。","technical_approach":"方法很简单。给定固定模型、固定层和输入文本，取该层所有 token 的 hidden states，形成一个 activation matrix。然后计算这个矩阵的奇异值谱，用相对阈值统计有多少个方向仍然接近主奇异方向。这个计数称为 D-Score，可理解为 hidden trajectory 的相对数值秩。直觉是：被模型内部知识支持的文本会形成更集中的表示轨迹，主要变化落在少数方向上；包含虚假或不确定 claim 的文本会引入冲突信号，使表示能量分散到更多方向。检测规则是在校准好的 tolerance 参数和决策阈值下，当 D-Score 超过阈值时判为 hallucinated。实现上不需要完整 SVD；只要逐步估计前几个奇异值，直到低于相对阈值即可，可用 power method 等近似算法。","experiments":"实验使用 Llama-2-7B、Llama-3-8B 和 Vicuna-7B，在多个 hallucination benchmark 上评估。作者报告 D-Score 在两类数据集、四个指标和三个开源模型上优于若干常见检测方法。可确认的比较对象包括依赖内部状态的检测方法，如 INSIDE、LLM-Check，以及常见的检索式或一致性式检测思路。实验的关键点不是模型规模，而是 D-Score 只需单次前向、单层 hidden states、无外部参考。需要注意的是，给出的文本没有包含具体表格数值、各层最优位置、阈值选择细节和误差条，因此不能据此判断提升幅度是否稳定。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 15 到 20 分钟。先看方法定义，确认 D-Score 的阈值方向、矩阵维度和奇异值截断实现。再看实验表格，重点关注三件事：不同层的敏感性、tolerance 和决策阈值如何校准、长度和文本类型是否被控制。限制部分也值得看，因为这种谱信号很容易受 token 数、句子结构、主题复杂度和模型层选择影响。若用于预训练或后训练诊断，需要额外验证它与事实性、置信度、困惑度、entropy、activation norm 等信号的关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether hallucinated or unsupported claims can be detected from the hidden-state geometry produced by a single forward pass. It avoids retrieval, repeated sampling, and an extra trained classifier. The central question is whether a model’s internal activation trajectory becomes spectrally less concentrated when processing a claim that conflicts with, or is not supported by, its own internal knowledge.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method paper, but it is relevant as an internal-state diagnostic. It suggests that reliability signals may be visible in the geometry of hidden activations, not only in output probabilities or sampled consistency. For foundation-model work, such a signal could be useful for offline monitoring during post-training, factuality evaluation, or analysis of calibration failures. The contribution should be read as a simple diagnostic proposal rather than a training objective: it does not yet establish robust generalization across all models, layers, lengths, and domains.","technical_approach":"For a fixed model and layer, the method stacks token-level hidden states into an activation matrix and examines its singular spectrum. The D-Score is a relative numerical-rank statistic: it counts how many singular directions remain sufficiently close to the leading direction under a chosen tolerance. A larger D-Score means that the hidden trajectory spreads its energy over more comparable directions. The motivation is that supported text should induce a more coherent trajectory, while unsupported or internally conflicting claims introduce additional directions associated with uncertainty or contradiction. The detector uses a calibrated tolerance and a decision threshold, and predicts hallucination when the D-Score exceeds that threshold. Computationally, a full singular decomposition is not required; leading singular values can be estimated until the relative cutoff is reached.","experiments":"The experiments are reported on Llama-2-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Vicuna-7B across multiple hallucination benchmarks. The paper claims stronger results than several common hallucination detectors over two datasets, four metrics, and three open-weight models. The important experimental property is that D-Score uses one forward pass, one layer, and no external evidence. The provided content does not include exact table values, layer-wise optima, threshold calibration details, or confidence intervals, so the safest reading is that the method is promising as a lightweight signal, while the magnitude and stability of the gains need to be checked directly in the tables.","reading_guide":"A 15-to-20-minute skim should cover the main idea. Read the method section carefully enough to verify the activation-matrix construction, the relative singular-value threshold, and the truncated SVD implementation. In the experiments, look for layer sensitivity, tolerance calibration, decision-threshold selection, and controls for sequence length or topic complexity. The limitations matter here: spectral rank can be affected by text length, syntactic diversity, domain shift, and the chosen layer. For pretraining or post-training diagnostics, the next useful step would be to compare D-Score with entropy, perplexity, activation norms, factuality labels, and self-consistency signals.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"基于隐藏激活谱的幻觉检测方法，思路简洁，可快速浏览方法部分，重点关注其在不同模型/任务上的泛化性。","en":"A concise hallucination detection method based on hidden activation spectra; skim the method section, focusing on its generalization across models and tasks."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Bologna","aDepartment of Computer Science and Engineering, University of Bologna, Bologna"],"authors":["Bianca Raimondi","Davide Evangelista","Maurizio Gabbrielli","Elena Loli Piccolomini"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["hallucination detection","interpretability","spectral analysis","hidden states","LLM"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24586","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.24586","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.23153","title":"In-Context Learning as Implicit Policy Gradient","abstract":"Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can iteratively improve their outputs by incorporating generated samples and their corresponding evaluation scores as in-context examples. Despite these empirical findings, the theoretical foundations underlying this phenomenon remain poorly understood. In this paper, we show that score-conditioned In-Context Learning (ICL) admits a structural correspondence to policy gradient optimization. We first provide a constructive proof that self-at","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-25T11:13:52Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_23153.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有上下文学习（ICL）的理论框架仅覆盖监督学习场景下输入输出对(x,y)的学习机制，无法解释分数条件ICL（模型利用自生成样本及其评价分数(y,r)对迭代优化输出，如OPRO、自奖励对齐等经验现象）背后的学习算法本质，存在理论空白。","importance":"建立了分数条件ICL与策略梯度优化的结构对应关系，为理解无参数更新下的奖励式上下文适应提供理论基础，对提示优化、自奖励对齐、上下文RL等方法的设计有指导意义。","technical_approach":"1. 构造性证明：在特定权重矩阵配置下，自注意力机制可实现类似REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合——注意力权重由样本分数而非输入相似度加权，在隐状态空间产生向高奖励输出的分数加权更新，结构上等价于REINFORCE策略梯度估计；2. 理论推导：注意力诱导的隐状态更新对输出分布的KL偏移存在精确上界，具有类似信任域的性质；3. 实验验证：在多个主流LLM上验证三个核心预测：样本分数与输出概率变化正相关、注意力权重与分数相关、ICL与显式REINFORCE微调的输出分布偏移方向一致。","experiments":"在Llama-3、Olmo-3、Qwen-3、GPT-4o、Gemini 2.5、Claude Sonnet 4共6个模型上完成验证，三个核心结论：1）上下文样本分数与模型输出概率变化呈正秩相关；2）注意力权重与样本分数存在相关性，为分数加权聚合提供机制层面证据；3）给定相同样本，ICL诱导的输出分布偏移与显式REINFORCE微调的偏移方向相似。","reading_guide":"重点看第3章的理论对应证明与第4章的实验验证部分，附录F补充了预训练模型行为的实证证据。预计阅读时间30-45分钟，关注构造性证明的假设条件与相关性分析结果即可，无需深究数学推导细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing theoretical frameworks for in-context learning (ICL) only explain learning from supervised input-output (x,y) pairs, leaving no principled account for score-conditioned ICL — where models iteratively improve outputs using self-generated samples and their evaluation scores (y,r) pairs, as seen in OPRO and self-rewarding alignment.","importance":"Establishes a structural correspondence between score-conditioned ICL and policy gradient optimization, providing a theoretical foundation for understanding reward-based in-context adaptation without parameter updates. Informs the design of prompt optimization, self-rewarding alignment, and in-context RL methods.","technical_approach":"First provides a constructive proof that under specific weight matrix configurations, self-attention implements reward-weighted aggregation analogous to the REINFORCE algorithm: attention weights scale by sample scores rather than input similarity, producing score-weighted updates toward high-reward outputs in hidden state space, structurally equivalent to the REINFORCE policy gradient estimator. Second proves that attention-induced hidden state updates yield an exact upper bound on KL divergence from the reference distribution, a trust-region-like property. Third validates three core predictions across multiple LLMs: positive correlation between sample scores and output probability changes, correlation between attention weights and scores, and aligned distribution shift direction between ICL and explicit REINFORCE fine-tuning given identical samples.","experiments":"Validated on 6 mainstream LLMs: Llama-3, Olmo-3, Qwen-3, GPT-4o, Gemini 2.5, and Claude Sonnet 4. Three core findings: (1) positive rank correlation between context sample scores and output probability changes; (2) correlation between attention weights and sample scores, providing mechanistic evidence for score-weighted aggregation; (3) ICL and explicit REINFORCE fine-tuning shift output distributions in similar directions given identical samples.","reading_guide":"Skim Section 3 for the core theoretical correspondence and Section 4 for experimental validation. We instead s","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-27T16:45:08Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_24645.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征的激活模式与其对模型输出的因果效应之间经常不一致：一个看起来语义清晰的特征，在不同上下文中被干预时可能产生弱、异质甚至方向相反的 logit 变化。","importance":"SAE 已成为 mechanistic interpretability 中最常用的特征分解工具，但“激活上可解释”不等于“行为上可稳定操控”。该文把分析重点从特征在模型内部的几何，推进到干预后经模型尾部传播到 logit 空间的效应几何，给出了 value-like 与 pointer-like 两类特征角色的区分。对关心表示结构、steering、以及通过 SAE 理解预训练特征如何参与下游计算的研究有方法论价值。","technical_approach":"提出 Feature-Effect Geometry Analysis（FEGA）。对给定层的某个 SAE 特征 j，在多个使其激活的上下文中，用 SAE 重建作为基线，将该特征的 latent 系数置零，得到特征消融后的激活；把基线和消融后的激活分别传入冻结的后续模型层和 logit 输出头，比较两者的 logit 差，构成该特征在多个上下文上的下游效应云 E_{j,logit}。在此基础上分析效应云的几何结构：是集中在一个共享方向（value-like，例如稳定实体知识），还是落在一个低维子空间并随上下文改变方向（pointer-like，例如在上下文中执行复制/规则应用的操作），还是呈扩散状无法低维解释。为避免 SAE 重建误差混淆，基线采用 SAE 重建而非原始激活。","experiments":"在多种 SAE 变体上，覆盖 RAVEL city-country 这类偏 value-like 的实体属性任务、Literal Sequence Copying / Word Content / PrOntoQA 这类偏 pointer-like 的合成和局部规则任务，以及 Token Translation 这类混合任务。实验表明同一 SAE 特征的下游效应在不同上下文并不总是对齐到单一 logit 方向：在实体知识类任务上更接近共享方向，在规则/复制类任务上表现为上下文依赖但结构化的低维效应空间，说明有些特征编码的是操作而非固定内容。这解释了为什么基于激活样例挑选的特征在 steering 时会失效，以及为什么某些干预在不同 prompt 下方向不一致。","reading_guide":"建议先看图1理解 FEGA 的干预与传播路径，再看 value-like vs pointer-like 的定义和对应任务上的效应云几何分析。重点关注为什么必须用 SAE reconstruction 作为基线、以及效应云低维结构如何对应到“编码概念”与“编码函数”两种角色。预计阅读时间 30-40 分钟。对做 SAE steering 或表示分析的读者，可以重点看方法部分，这部分是可以直接迁移到自己模型上的诊断工具。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Activation-based interpretability of sparse autoencoder features does not reliably predict their causal behavioral effects; features with clear semantic descriptions can produce weak, context-dependent, or even directionally opposite changes in model outputs when intervened on across prompts.","importance":"SAEs have become the standard tool for decomposing LLM internals into interpretable latents, but interpretability workflows that select features by activation patterns or textual descriptions often fail at steering. This work shifts the unit of analysis from feature geometry at the SAE site to the geometry of downstream logit changes after feature ablation, and proposes a distinction between value-like and pointer-like feature roles. This is methodologically relevant for anyone using SAEs for representation analysis, model steering, or studying how pretrained features are consumed by later layers.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce Feature-Effect Geometry Analysis (FEGA). For a given SAE feature j at layer ℓ, they collect contexts where the feature is active, ablate the feature by zeroing its latent coefficient, and compare the downstream logits produced by the feature-ablated SAE reconstruction versus the SAE reconstruction baseline. Baselines use the SAE reconstruction rather than raw activations to avoid confounding feature ablation with reconstruction error. The resulting per-context logit differences form a downstream effect cloud E_{j,logit}, whose geometry is analyzed: concentrated along a shared direction for value-like features (e.g., stable factual knowledge), lying in a low-dimensional but context-dependent subspace for pointer-like features (e.g., copying or rule-following operations), or diffuse when no simple structure exists.","experiments":"FEGA is applied across multiple SAE variants on tasks spanning value-like settings such as RAVEL city-country entity-attribute disentanglement, pointer-like synthetic and prompt-local-rule settings such as literal sequence copying, word content tasks, and PrOntoQA, plus the mixed setting of token translation. The results show that downstream effects of the same SAE feature do not universally align to a single logit direction. Entity-like features are closer to a shared effect direction, while operational features in rule/copy settings produce structured low-dimensional effect subspaces whose direction varies with context. This helps explain why activation-selected features can mislead steering and why interventions can reverse direction across prompts.","reading_guide":"Start with Figure 1 to understand the intervention path from SAE ablation through the frozen model tail to logits, then focus on the value-like versus pointer-like distinction and the corresponding effect-cloud geometry across tasks. Multilingual capability is often explained by data volume, tokenizer coverage, or benchmark contamination. This work adds another axis: whether the model organizes languages in representation space in a way that reflects their hierarchical similarity. That matters for multilingual data mixture design, shared tokenization, low-resource language scaling, and representation evaluation. A model that places related languages near one another is better positioned to transfer across them than a model whose language representations are fragmented, even if both have seen multilingual text.","technical_approach":"The authors frame the analysis using similarity-based generalization from cognitive science: generalization follows the geometry of the representation space. They examine twelve LLMs and derive language-level representations by aggregating latent activations over documents in each language, rather than relying on isolated word embeddings or vocabulary overlap. They then compute distances between languages in each model’s representation space and compare the induced structure with an external Indo-European language hierarchy derived from lexico-statistical phylogenetic work. The reference tree is used as a measure of hierarchical similarity, not as a strict claim about historical descent. The final step links this representational alignment to multilingual benchmark performance.","experiments":"The experiments compare twelve LLMs on Indo-European languages. The key findings are qualitative from the available content. First, LLM latent representations recover a substantial amount of hierarchical language structure without explicit supervision over language families. Second, models that better preserve this language-level similarity structure also tend to perform better on multilingual benchmarks. The supplied material does not include exact correlations, model-by-model tables, or ablation numbers, so the result is best read as a representation-analysis study rather than as direct evidence for a specific pretraining recipe.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim should focus on the representation construction and the evaluation metric. Check which layers are used, how document representations are pooled, how language distances are computed, and whether the analysis controls for script, vocabulary overlap, and training-data imbalance. The most important empirical section is the comparison between each model’s induced language structure and the reference Indo-European hierarchy, followed by the correlation with multilingual benchmark performance. The useful takeaway for pretraining work is the diagnostic idea: multilingual evaluation should inspect the geometry of language representations, not only final task accuracy.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从语言相似性视角解释多语言泛化，发现表示空间与谱系结构对应，对多语言预训练的数据配比有启发，看§3和Fig 2即可。","en":"Explains multilingual generalization via language similarity in representation space; informative for multilingual pretraining data design, read §3 and Fig 2."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Supantho Rakshit","Adele Goldberg","Henry Conklin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["multilingual LLM","generalization mechanism","representation analysis","language similarity","interpretability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22699","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.22699","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.23055","title":"SymStep: Symbolic Step Verification for Logical Reasoning","abstract":"Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting can fail severely on constraint-dense logical reasoning tasks, where unverified errors accumulate silently across steps. 提出一个原子断言，符号系统负责校验和传播”。这个设计说明，在某些离散约束任务上，主要瓶颈不一定是语言模型缺少某条知识，而是缺少可靠的状态维护和搜索方向。对后续 SFT/RL 数据设计也有启发：训练信号可以落在原子动作、外部 verifier 反馈、以及下一变量选择上，而不是只奖励最终答案。","technical_approach":"方法核心是一个逐步交互协议。LLM 每轮只输出一个可正则解析的 state, and lack of search direction even when individual deductions are correct.","importance":"The paper is not a pretraining paper, but it is useful for thinking about verifiable reasoning interfaces and data generation for process supervision. It replaces free-form CoT with a loop where the model proposes one atomic claim and a symbolic component maintains the state. The main lesson is that, for discrete constraint problems, the bottleneck may be state tracking and variable selection rather than missing knowledge. This has implications for SFT/RL data: supervision can be attached to atomic actions, verifier feedback, and next-variable selection instead of only final answers.","technical_approach":"The system asks the LLM to emit one regex-parseable Deduce assertion per turn, such as DEDUCE: Alice, pet, Cat. A lightweight constraint propagator checks whether the claim is consistent with prior accepted facts and the original constraints. Accepted claims update the state and may trigger automatic cascaded eliminations through arc-consistency-style propagation. Rejected claims receive an explicit contradiction. SymStep+G adds MRV guidance: after each accepted step, it points the model to the unresolved variable with the fewest remaining candidates. This differs from Logic-LM or LINC-style pipelines that translate the whole problem into formal logic in one shot. It also differs from LLM-as-verifier methods because the verification decision is deterministic and external to the model.","experiments":"The strongest results are on constraint-dense benchmarks. It then performs representation steering along that direction and measures the effect with a refusal concept activation score. The observed effect is that steering along representative scenario directions reduces refusal, similar to applying the actual scenario wrapper in text. Building on this, Concept2Scenario decomposes internal activations with a sparse autoencoder, intervenes on individual concept directions, measures their causal effect on refusal, interprets high-activation examples, and translates the resulting concepts into actionable natural-language scenarios. The method also includes interaction attribution to identify synergistic combinations of scenario concepts without exhaustively enumerating all combinations.","experiments":"The experiments span three open-source models, two safety benchmarks, and six representative black-box jailbreak methods. Scenarios discovered by Concept2Scenario act as reusable priors for different jailbreak methods and improve average attack success rates by up to 18.2 percentage points on open-source models. Scenarios discovered from open-source models also transfer to closed-source systems, including GPT-5, Claude-Haiku-4.5, and Gemini-3-Flash. Interaction attribution finds multi-scenario combinations that outperform individual scenarios and reduce the number of attack turns. 控制。","importance":"这篇论文对基座预训练的价值在训练动态和正则化理解。它给出一个可解机制：在训练集上不可见、但在总体分布上可见的参数方向，会在 weight decay 的慢恢复力下驱动晚期泛化改善。它不能直接解释大模型预训练中的所有 grokking-like 现象，因为假设包括平方损失、full-batch heavy-ball、弱正则和局部二次近似；但它提供了清晰的因果预测，例如延迟时间随 (1-β)/(ηλ) 缩放。这类结论有助于理解 weight decay、momentum 和训练后期 cooldown/annealing 对泛化尾部的影响。","technical_approach":"核心几何对象是经验 Gram 矩阵 G_N 和总体 Gram 矩阵 G_pop 的核空间关系。由于 Ker G_pop 包含在 Ker G_N 中，训练集上不改变预测的方向可以分成两类：一类在总体分布上也不可见，另一类在训练集上不可见但在总体上仍然改变预测。作者把后一类称为 grokking subspace。在线性参数模型、平方损失、full-batch heavy-ball 加 coupled L2 regularization 的设置下，训练递推的 mode spectrum 可以精确求出。沿经验核空间的平移是训练损失的对称性，weight decay 软性破坏这个对称性，于是产生慢恢复动力学。论文进一步给出离散时间和连续时间的慢根表达式，并用 soft Noether law 描述慢坐标的守恒破缺；非线性网络部分只做局部二次扩展，并显式说明误差项和适用条件。","experiments":"理论给出的主要时间尺度是 k_grok ≈ (1-β)/(ηλ) grokking subspace 和真正 population-null 方向的 train/test 行为；还观察到 coupled L2 与 decoupled weight decay 之间存在 factor-(1-β) 的差异。第二层是 modular addition benchmark，出现真实 delayed generalization，延迟随 (1-β)/(ηλ) 缩放；再看 Theorem 20，理解为什么只有 population-active empirical-null 方向贡献晚期 population risk tail。实验部分优先看 Section 7 的 synthetic intervention 和 modular addition 缩放验证。限制条件也值得看：该机制不解释无显式 weight decay 的 grokking、不解释 feature learning 产生新总体可见方向、不处理 mini-batch noise 和 adaptive optimizer。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies the late-time dynamics of grokking: test loss can keep improving long after training loss has effectively converged. The central question is not whether weight decay induces a slow timescale, but which part of the post-interpolation motion affects population risk and how that motion depends on heavy-ball momentum, learning rate, and weight decay.","importance":"The paper is useful for training-dynamics research. It provides a solvable mechanism in which directions invisible on the training set but visible under the population distribution control the late generalization tail. The result is not a direct model of large-scale LLM pretraining, since it assumes squared loss, full-batch heavy-ball dynamics, weak regularization, and locally quadratic behavior for nonlinear networks. Still, the scaling prediction k_grok proportional to (1-β)/(ηλ) is a concrete handle on how weight decay and momentum can set late-time generalization clocks.","technical_approach":"The key object is the mismatch between the empirical Gram matrix G_N and the population Gram matrix G_pop. The null spaces satisfy Ker G_pop ⊆ Ker G_N. Directions in Ker G_N do not change training predictions, but only the quotient component that remains active under G_pop can change population predictions. The authors call this component the grokking subspace. For models linear in parameters, trained with squared loss using full-batch heavy-ball optimization and coupled L2 regularization, they derive the exact discrete-time mode spectrum. Translations along the empirical null space are symmetries of the training loss; weight decay softly breaks these symmetries, producing a slow restoring dynamics. The paper also gives continuous-time and Hamiltonian interpretations through a soft Noether law, then extends the picture to nonlinear networks under a local quadratic approximation with explicit remainder assumptions.","experiments":"The main prediction is k_grok ≈ (1-β)/(ηλ) log(||Π_g θ_0||/ε), where Π_g θ_0 is the initial amplitude in the grokking subspace. The first empirical test uses an exactly computable synthetic model and verifies, without fitted parameters, the collapse of relaxation counts under (1-β)/(ηλ). It also shows logarithmic delay shifts when the grokking component is rescaled while training predictions are preserved, separates the signature of the grokking subspace from genuinely population-null directions, and confirms a factor-(1-β) difference between coupled L2 and decoupled weight decay. A modular-addition benchmark then exhibits delayed generalization with a measured log-log slope of 1.01 for the predicted scaling.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 35–50 minute skim with selective close reading. Start with the definitions of Ker G_N, Ker G_pop, and the grokking subspace. Then read Theorem 4 and Corollary 3.1 for the exact slow root and the iteration-count scaling under heavy-ball dynamics. Theorem 20 is the main generalization statement: only the population-active empirical-null component contributes to the slow population-risk tail. Section 7 is the most useful experimental part, especially the synthetic interventions and the modular-addition scaling check. The limitations are important: the mechanism does not cover grokking without explicit weight decay, feature-learning creation of new population-visible directions, mini-batch noise, abrupt accuracy jumps, or adaptive optimizers.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从线性模型解析推导grokking的权重衰减驱动机制，给出精确缩放律，对理解优化器与正则化的交互有参考，重点看§3核心结论。","en":"Analytically derives weight-decay-driven grokking mechanism in linear models with exact scaling, useful for understanding optimizer-regularization interaction; focus on §3 core results."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Taeyoung Kim"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["grokking","optimization","weight_decay","generalization","training_dynamics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23967","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.23967","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.23050","title":"The Entropic Bound for Transformers: Why Static Rank Fails and Attention-Native Rank Recovers","abstract":"Neural scaling laws describe how loss decreases as models, data, and compute grow, but they do not answer a prior question: for a fixed task, what is the minimum model capacity required to solve it? The harness is treated as part of the data-generating process, not just inference-time scaffolding. If the harness has ambiguous prompts, broken tool schemas, missing retry logic, or poor context management, the resulting trajectories contain avoidable noise. Improving the harness before collecting new training data can change the quality of the learning signal, which is relevant to SFT, RL, and automated data pipelines for agents.","technical_approach":"Co-Harness formulates agent improvement as joint optimization over model parameters θ and a harness configuration φ. Since direct joint optimization is expensive and rewards are sparse, the method alternates between harness optimization and model optimization. With the model fixed, the system collects rollouts and sends failed trajectories to an LLM-based HarnessCritic. The critic attributes failures to structured harness-level causes, such as prompt ambiguity, tool-schema mismatch, missing skills, or middleware problems, and proposes local diffs. A diff is accepted only if validation rollouts improve the targeted failure mode without regressing held-out behavior. Each argument of a tool call is assigned a semantic role (e.g., target, credential, command, content) with its own risk budget and threshold, performing Mondrian (class-conditional) conformal calibration at the role level. This resolves the dilution problem of aggregate calibration: under aggregate control, the violation rate of a rare role r is inflated by 1/p_r (where p_r is role prevalence), so guaranteeing role-specific risk α requires shrinking the effective budget to α·p_r, while stratified calibration assigns independent budgets directly to each role. The method also supports pooling for rare roles, label-conditional calibration (providing prevalence-invariant attack-conditional guarantees), and recovers detector-relative noninterference in the zero-budget limit. It operates as a wrapper layer compatible with any per-field detector, and improves in lockstep with detector performance.","experiments":"Evaluated across 6 models and 2 benchmarks. Empirical utility gaps align with the theoretically predicted \"price of coarseness\". The role-stratified method remains risk-compliant under distribution shift, unseen test suites, detector noise, gradual drift, and adaptive attacks. A 2×2 ablation shows stratification delivers the primary robustness gain, while conformal recalibration restores finite-sample validity under new conditions.","reading_guide":"Skim the problem setup & threat model, theoretical method sections, and ablation experiments. Focus on understanding the theoretical justification for role stratification and its practical effectiveness. GUI agent 的一个老问题：高质量任务轨迹贵、RL 交互训练重，而直接用 instruction-conditioned trajectory fine-tuning 又把视觉定位、界面动态理解、动作语义和任务规划全压进一个 next-action objective 里，导致数据效率和优化都不理想。作者要解决的是，能不能在不依赖额外任务级标注的前提下，先用更便宜、更容易扩展的 state transition 数据，把 GUI 交互里最基础的 action-grounded visual representation 和局部 dynamics 先学出来，再去做任务轨迹微调。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接相关性不算最高，因为它主要是 GUI agent 训练范式，而不是通用 LLM 预训练。但它有两个值得注意的点。第一，它把“状态转移建模”明确当成一个独立于 trajectory SFT 的 scaling axis，这个思路和语言模型里把局部结构学习与高层任务对齐解耦是相通的。第二，它采用 unified multimodal model 同时做 image-text-to-image 的 forward dynamics 和 image-image-to-text 的 inverse dynamics，这种把生成式 world modeling 与 action recovery 结合的预训练方式，对多模态 agent pretraining 有启发。若从更广义的 pretrain/post-train 接口看，这篇论文提供了一个例子：先用廉价、局部、密集监督学环境动力学，再用稀缺、长程、任务级数据学规划。","technical_approach":"方法叫 State Transition Pretraining，简称 STP。训练样本是单步转移三元组 (s_t, a_t, s_{t+1})，可以从已有轨迹切出来，也可以在交互环境中自动收集。STP 有两个联合目标。其一是 forward dynamics：输入当前界面状态和动作，预测下一界面状态，相当于 image-text-to-image 建模。作者希望模型借此形成 GUI dynamics 的内部表征，知道点击、输入、滚动之后界面会怎么变。其二是 inverse dynamics：输入前后两个截图，预测中间发生的动作，相当于 image-image-to-text 建模。这个目标迫使模型关注真正发生交互的位置和变化区域，从而学到更细粒度、与动作相关的视觉特征。论文的一个关键选择是使用 unified multimodal model 作为 backbone，而不是把视觉编码、动作分类、状态预测拆成多个专用模块。这样 forward 和 inverse 两个目标可以在统一 token 空间里共享表示，也更容易无缝衔接后续 trajectory fine-tuning。作者强调 STP 不是替代任务微调，而是提供更好的初始化。与直接 trajectory SFT 相比，它把局部可学习结构单独拿出来，减少 instruction-following、planning、grounding、dynamics 几种能力在同一损失里互相干扰。消融部分还表明，forward 和 inverse 是互补的：只做其中一个都有收益，但联合训练最好；另外，transition 按完整轨迹采样还是按独立 step 采样，效果接近，说明收益主要来自状态转移监督本身，而不是序列组织方式。","experiments":"主实验覆盖桌面和移动端 GUI benchmark。桌面端在 AgentNetBench 上，在相同 trajectory fine-tuning 配置下，加入 STP 后平均 success rate 提升 2.3% 到 6.2%，coordinate success rate 提升 1.6% 到 6.8%，提升幅度会随 fine-tuning data split 而变化。移动端在 AndroidControl 上，step success rate 提升 0.9%，coordinate prediction accuracy 提升 1.3%；在 GUIOdyssey 上，step success rate 提升 0.6%，coordinate prediction accuracy 提升 1.6%。论文还给出优化层面的证据：Figure 1 左图显示 STP 初始化能改善后续 trajectory fine-tuning 的 cross-entropy loss 曲线，说明它不只是多看了些数据，而是让优化更顺。可扩展性实验有两条。第一，在固定 fine-tuning 数据时，增加 transition pretraining 数据会稳定提升下游表现，说明 STP 确实是一条可扩展的数据轴。第二，在固定 STP 设置时，把 trajectory fine-tuning 数据从 2K 扩到 18K，STP 仍持续优于 fine-tune-only baseline，说明收益不是简单的数据重复曝光，而是来自 forward/inverse dynamics 提供的结构化监督。对预训练研究者来说，这组结果最有价值的不是绝对分数，而是“便宜的局部转移监督可以持续改善昂贵的任务级训练”这个趋势。","reading_guide":"建议先读 Introduction 里对 direct trajectory fine-tuning 两个瓶颈的拆解：数据稀缺和目标耦合。然后直接看 STP 方法部分，重点理解为什么 forward dynamics 和 inverse dynamics 要一起做，而不是只做 action prediction。接着看主结果表和 Figure 1 左图，确认它既带来最终指标提升，也改善优化过程。最后看 scalability 和 ablation：这两部分决定它是不是一个真正独立的 scaling axis，而不只是额外预训练带来的偶然增益。若只是泛读，30 分钟左右足够。最值得留意的点有三个：统一多模态 backbone 的训练接口、forward/inverse 的互补性、以及 step-level transition 数据是否能替代部分昂贵轨迹监督。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a bottleneck in GUI-agent training: instruction-conditioned trajectory fine-tuning is expensive to scale and mixes several hard subproblems into one next-action objective. A model must learn visual grounding, local GUI dynamics, action semantics, and task planning all at once, while high-quality trajectory data remains costly. The paper asks whether a cheaper and denser supervision source—single-step visual state transitions—can be used as a separate pretraining stage to learn action-grounded representations and local environment dynamics before task-level fine-tuning.","importance":"This is not core LLM pretraining in the narrow text-only sense, but it is relevant as a multimodal pretraining recipe for agents. The paper is also interesting because it uses a unified multimodal model to jointly learn forward dynamics and inverse dynamics, combining world-model-like prediction with action recovery.
